AutoMaster: Autonomous Post-Training by Making the Same Model a Better Teacher
AutoMaster：通过使同一模型成为更优秀的教师实现自主后训练
Task success alone does not specify which behaviors post-training should reinforce. Even successful teacher trajectories can contain unsupported assumptions, unproductive repetition, or superficial verification. We propose Skill-FT (Skill-Guided Fine-Tuning), which uses natural-language skills to suppress undesirable habits and elicit underrepresented effective behaviors during teacher rollouts, then fine-tunes a target model on the resulting trajectories. We introduce AutoMaster, an autonomous post-training system in which an LLM agent develops these interventions through trajectory analysis, skill design, data selection, and feedback from rollouts and target-model evaluation. Across Terminal, Search, and software engineering tasks with 4B and 9B target models, AutoMaster improves mean performance over Vanilla SFT on successful unguided teacher trajectories by 1.42-8.99 percentage points, with the teacher, task pool, training set size, and SFT recipe held fixed. Together with the ablations and trajectory analyses, these results suggest that behavioral shaping can make a fixed model a more effective teacher. We further find that retaining skill instructions during SFT yields higher mean performance than removing them across all six settings, despite their absence at evaluation and identical supervised outputs. These findings suggest that preserving skill context as part of a training demonstration can support better behavioral internalization.
中文翻译
仅靠任务成功无法明确后训练应强化哪些行为。即使成功的教师轨迹也可能包含未经证实的假设、无益的重复或表面验证。我们提出Skill-FT（技能引导微调），利用自然语言技能在教师回放过程中抑制不良习惯并激发未充分代表的有效行为，随后在生成的轨迹上对目标模型进行微调。我们引入AutoMaster，一个自主后训练系统，其中大语言模型代理通过轨迹分析、技能设计、数据选择以及回放和目标模型评估的反馈来开发这些干预措施。在终端、搜索和软件工程任务中，使用4B和9B的目标模型，AutoMaster在保持教师、任务池、训练集大小和SFT方案不变的情况下，相较于原始SFT在成功无指导教师轨迹上的平均性能提升了1.42至8.99个百分点。结合消融实验和轨迹分析，这些结果表明行为塑造可以使固定模型成为更有效的教师。我们还发现，在SFT过程中保留技能指令比移除它们能获得更高的平均性能，尽管评估阶段这些指令不存在且监督输出完全相同。这些发现表明，将技能上下文作为训练示范的一部分有助于更好的行为内化。
👤 Guoxin Chen, Shuang Sun, Fanzhe Meng, Xinyu Tang, Ruihua Song et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

Beam Model Technical Report
Beam模型技术报告
Beam is a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model with 501.3B total parameters and 23.4B active parameters, specifically built and optimized for coding, reasoning, and agentic workloads. Beam delivers frontier level reasoning, while requiring 3-4x less inference compute than many models of its class. Pretrained on 23.8T diverse, curated, high-quality tokens, Beam features a 1M-token context window achieved through a novel two-stage midtraining phase, built on verifiable, correctness-anchored data. Post-training leveraged a large-scale, fully asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) campaign across 10.5K GB300 GPUs, generating over 100M rollouts over 4 weeks of training. We believe this is one of the largest scale RL runs conducted by any open lab to date. Across our evaluation suite, capabilities continued to improve as we increased RL compute, with no sign of a plateau. Supported by deep hardware-model co-design, FP8/NVFP4 quantization, and a 1.93B-parameter speculative decoding drafter (DFlash 2), Beam maximizes serving throughput with efficient, flexible deployment. Averaged across conversation turns and harm categories, Beam refuses or pivots on 88.9% of harmful requests while complying with 81.5% of harmless requests, achieving the highest refusal/pivot rate on harmful requests among open-weight models on the Pareto frontier.
中文翻译
Beam是一个稀疏专家混合（MoE）模型，总参数量达5013亿，活跃参数为234亿，专为编程、推理和智能体工作负载构建并优化。Beam在推理能力上达到前沿水平，同时所需的推理计算量仅为同类模型的1/3至1/4。其在23.8万亿个多样化、精选且高质量的token上进行预训练，通过一种新颖的两阶段中期训练阶段实现了100万token的上下文窗口，该阶段基于可验证、以正确性为核心的训练数据。后训练阶段利用大规模、全异步强化学习（RL）训练，在10,500块GB300 GPU上运行，历时四周，生成超过1亿次回放。我们认为这是迄今为止任何开源实验室开展的最大规模强化学习训练之一。在我们的评估套件中，随着强化学习计算资源的增加，模型能力持续提升，未见性能饱和迹象。得益于深度软硬件协同设计、FP8/NVFP4量化以及一个19.3亿参数的推测解码起草器（DFlash 2），Beam实现了高吞吐量的服务部署，具备高效灵活的特性。在对话轮次和有害类别上的平均表现显示，Beam对88.9%的有害请求予以拒绝或转向，对81.5%的无害请求予以遵守，是当前开放权重模型中在有害请求拒绝/转向率方面位于帕累托前沿的最高水平。
👤 Reflection Team, Adam Abdulhamid, Osama Abuelsorour, Yasmin Alameddine, Carlo Alfano et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

One Framework to Diagnose Them All: A Stratified Benchmark for Bengali LLM Hallucination
一个框架诊断所有问题：针对孟加拉语大语言模型幻觉的分层基准
Hallucination research in low-resource languages fragments easily: each newly named failure mode prompts its own dataset, spending scarce annotators on incomparable resources. We argue for consolidation and test it at community scale. Alikbachan is a Bengali hallucination-detection benchmark stratified along three crossed axes: a human-validated cultural-distance band, six task types, and a five-family, 32-mode hallucination taxonomy. It comprises a harvested 2,516-item test set (Phase 1) and a quota-constructed, gold-labeled 5,000-item set (Phase 2). The stratification is diagnostic. Four zero-shot LLM judges vary by over 30 macro-F1 points across strata, and the item-level predictions of 99 Phase-1 and 32 Phase-2 competition systems reproduce the profile: discourse-level omission defeats judges and most teams alike, whereas only a minority over-flag negative controls. The judges' verdict probabilities rank items well (ROC-AUC 0.74-0.95) but are overconfident (ECE 0.04-0.29). An open competition on Alikbachan drew 222 teams and 3,566 submissions, and a single-prompt 32B judge reaches the 57th percentile. Of the 25 teams in both phases, 24 regress on Phase 2 (mean $-17.6$ points) while every zero-shot judge improves, and Phase-1 rank barely predicts Phase-2 rank (Spearman $ρ=0.35$), so a Phase-1 leaderboard alone would have crowned a different winner.
中文翻译
低资源语言中的幻觉研究容易碎片化：每一种新出现的错误模式都会催生独立的数据集，导致稀缺标注者投入不可比的资源。我们主张整合并在此社区规模上进行验证。Alikbachan 是一个针对孟加拉语幻觉检测的分层基准，沿三个交叉维度划分：经人工验证的文化距离区间、六种任务类型以及涵盖五类家族、32种模式的幻觉分类体系。该基准包含一个采集的2,516项测试集（第一阶段）和一个配额构建、黄金标注的5,000项数据集（第二阶段）。分层设计具有诊断意义：四种零样本大语言模型裁判在不同分层上的宏F1得分差异超过30点，99个第一阶段与32个第二阶段竞赛系统的逐项预测结果也重现了这一特征： discourse 级别的遗漏问题击败了裁判和大多数团队，而仅有少数系统过度标记负向对照。裁判的判决概率表现良好（ROC-AUC 0.74–0.95），但过于自信（ECE 0.04–0.29）。在 Alikbachan 上举办的开放竞赛吸引了222支队伍和3,566次提交，单提示32B裁判达到第57百分位。在两个阶段均参与的25支队伍中，24支在第二阶段得分下降（平均-17.6分），而所有零样本裁判均有所提升，且第一阶段排名几乎无法预测第二阶段排名（Spearman ρ=0.35），因此仅凭第一阶段排行榜将选出不同的优胜者。
👤 Asif Shahriyar Sushmit, Shefayat E Shams Adib, Abdullah Al Jubaer Gem, Ahmed Shafin Ruhan, MD. Nazmuddoha Ansary et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

SheetAlign: Grounded Interaction and Stage-Isolated Learning for Spreadsheet Agents
SheetAlign：面向电子表格智能体的具身交互与阶段隔离学习
Spreadsheets are widely used for data analysis and supporting decision-making. Automating spreadsheet analysis while protecting sensitive data and controlling deployment costs motivates locally deployed open-weight large language models (LLMs). Meanwhile, complex spreadsheet tasks require both task reasoning and workbook understanding, while predefined operation interfaces impose additional tool-selection and invocation demands. We introduce SheetAlign, a spreadsheet manipulation framework with stage-isolated policy learning, organized around where to operate, what to change, and how to implement transformations. Task-guided inspection and API grounding anchor code generation in workbook observations and available operations, while stage-isolated learning optimizes program generation and review using edit-progress rewards. At inference, execution consistency selects programs through agreement among workbook states. On SpreadsheetBench, our grounded task interface improves Qwen3-4B's accuracy from 13.5% to 29.2% on average without training. With learning and execution consistency, SheetAlign achieves 32.8% accuracy on average on SpreadsheetBench and 30.1% on Domain-Spreadsheet, outperforming comparable baselines. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.
中文翻译
电子表格广泛用于数据分析和决策支持。在保护敏感数据并控制部署成本的前提下，自动化电子表格分析推动了本地部署的开源权重大语言模型（LLMs）的发展。同时，复杂的电子表格任务既需要任务推理，也需要对工作簿的理解，而预定义的操作接口则带来了额外的工具选择和调用需求。我们提出了SheetAlign，一种以操作位置、修改内容和实现转换方式为核心组织的电子表格操作框架，采用阶段隔离策略学习。任务引导的检查和API具身化将代码生成锚定在工作簿观测结果和可用操作上，而阶段隔离学习则利用编辑进度奖励优化程序生成与审查。在推理阶段，通过工作簿状态的一致性选择程序。在SpreadsheetBench基准上，我们的具身任务接口使Qwen3-4B的准确率从13.5%提升至29.2%（无需训练）。结合学习与执行一致性，SheetAlign在SpreadsheetBench上平均达到32.8%的准确率，在Domain-Spreadsheet上达到30.1%，优于可比基线。代码将在发表后公开。
👤 Yuxiang Wang, Junhao Gan, Jianzhong Qi 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

Web search in Latin America: language models use less local and diverse news than search engines
拉丁美洲的网络搜索：语言模型使用的本地和多样化新闻少于搜索引擎
AI platforms are becoming a major gateway to news and current events. This shift risks amplifying existing geographical biases that favour narratives from wealthier countries over those from the Global South. This is particularly relevant in Latin America, which accounts for the largest share of AI usage in the Global South. We examine how information about Latin America changes when users shift from conventional search engines to LLM-based search. We construct a dataset of 1,901 current events across nine Latin American countries, craft realistic queries, and submit them to three search engines and four language models. We vary query language and user location to represent local, diaspora, tourist, and foreign user scenarios. Across 49,207 requests and 8,834 unique domains, language models return a less diverse set of news sources than search engines. Across user types, the share of domestic news returned by language models is inconsistent. For local users, while search engines already return only 41.3\% local news, language models return a further 3.6 to 11.6 percentage points less. Taken together, our findings suggest that LLM-based search can reproduce and, in some settings, amplify geographical hierarchies in the reach and visibility of news sources. We release an open dataset to support multilingual and geographically diverse evaluation of LLM-based search, and highlight implications for more equitable information systems design.
中文翻译
AI平台正成为获取新闻和时事的主要入口。这一转变可能加剧现有地理偏见，使富裕国家的叙事优于全球南方国家的叙事。这一点在拉丁美洲尤为突出，该地区占全球南方AI使用量的最大份额。我们研究了当用户从传统搜索引擎转向基于大语言模型（LLM）的搜索时，关于拉丁美洲的信息如何变化。我们构建了一个涵盖九个拉丁美洲国家的1,901个当前事件的数据集，设计了真实查询，并向三个搜索引擎和四个语言模型提交请求。通过改变查询语言和用户位置，模拟本地、侨民、游客及外国用户场景。在49,207次请求和8,834个唯一域名中，语言模型返回的新闻来源多样性低于搜索引擎。在不同用户类型中，语言模型返回的本地新闻比例不一致。对于本地用户，尽管搜索引擎已仅返回41.3%的本地新闻，语言模型进一步减少了3.6至11.6个百分点。综合来看，我们的发现表明，基于LLM的搜索可能复制甚至在某些情况下放大新闻来源在传播和可见性上的地理层级。我们发布了开放数据集，以支持多语言和地理多样性的LLM搜索评估，并强调对更公平信息体系设计的意义。
👤 Francisco W. Kerche, Franziska Sofia Hafner, Felix M. Simon, Luc Rocher 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

Verifiable Logic in Medical LLMs: A Critical Review of Agentic Clinical Reasoning Optimization and Neuro-Symbolic Grounding
医疗大语言模型中的可验证逻辑：代理式临床推理优化与神经符号基础的批判性综述
Clinical large language models are usually judged by whether the final answer is right. Yet a model can reach a fluent, plausible output through invalid reasoning that static benchmarks do not detect, and confident hallucination, causally invalid inference, and spurious demographic shortcuts can pass conventional evaluation. This critical review covers four paradigms in turn: how clinical hallucination arises and why static metrics miss it; the agentic hallucination cascade, in which multi-agent and tool-augmented systems propagate and amplify early reasoning errors; process reward modeling as step-level supervision; and neuro-symbolic grounding, in which formal logic engines, ontology reasoners, and knowledge graphs place verifiable constraints on neural output. Within their encoded domains, systems with symbolic veto layers offer guarantees that hold relative to their encoded knowledge, and they report large gains on the safety measures they use. These results come from single studies, mostly in simulated or small-sample settings, and are not directly comparable with neural baselines. Process reward models remain bounded by the statistics of their training data. We propose a conceptual framework that maps each agentic failure mode to a symbolic intervention point, and we argue that clinical AI safety depends substantially on system architecture. Because current regulation emphasizes performance and does not require verification of reasoning steps, we argue that responsible certification should also audit reasoning trajectories alongside accuracy on static benchmarks.
中文翻译
临床大语言模型通常根据最终答案是否正确来评判。然而，模型可能通过无效推理产生流畅且看似合理的输出，而静态基准无法检测此类问题；自信的幻觉、因果无效推断以及虚假的人口统计学捷径可能通过常规评估。本文批判性综述了四个范式：临床幻觉的成因及其为何被静态指标忽视；多智能体与工具增强系统中出现的代理幻觉级联，即早期推理错误被传播并放大；过程奖励建模作为步骤级监督；以及神经符号基础，即形式逻辑引擎、本体推理器和知识图谱为神经输出施加可验证约束。在编码领域内，带有符号否决层的系统能提供相对于其编码知识的保证，并在所用安全指标上取得显著提升。这些成果来自单个研究，大多在模拟或小样本设置中，无法直接与神经基线比较。过程奖励模型仍受限于其训练数据的统计特性。我们提出一个概念框架，将每种代理失败模式映射到一个符号干预点，并认为临床AI安全在很大程度上取决于系统架构。由于当前监管强调性能而不强制验证推理步骤，我们主张负责任的认证也应审计推理轨迹，而不仅仅是静态基准的准确性。
👤 Youssef ElNahas, Adel Ammar, Soumaya Chaffar, Wadii Boulila 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

HALTT: A HAte Lexicon Through Time
HALTT：时间维度的仇恨言论词典
Automated hate speech detection degrades over time as everyday vocabulary is weaponized into covert slurs while retaining original benign meanings (polysemy retention). Existing lexical semantic change detection (LSCD) frameworks struggle with this phenomenon due to a lack of standardized, sense-delineated diachronic resources. We propose a multi-stage framework to construct and validate a diachronic lexicon of semantically shifted hate speech terms. Filtering > 20,000 candidates, our pipeline combines historical (1770-1964) and modern (2001-2024) corpus frequency grounding, masked definition-level scoring to mitigate LLM temporal bias, and a dual-persona reasoning prompt paradigm. We release HALTT, a grounded lexicon of 19 polysemous terms with literal and derogatory senses, analyze shift typologies, and establish evaluation protocols for downstream diachronic benchmarking.
中文翻译
随着日常词汇被转化为隐蔽的侮辱性用语而保留原始良性含义（多义性保留），自动化仇恨言论检测效果随时间下降。现有词汇语义变迁检测（LSCD）框架难以应对这一现象，因其缺乏标准化、意义分明的时间序列资源。我们提出一个多阶段框架，用于构建和验证语义变迁的仇恨言论术语时间词典。经过筛选超过20,000个候选词，我们的流程结合了历史（1770–1964）与现代（2001–2024）语料库的频率依据，采用掩码定义级评分以缓解LLM的时间偏差，并引入双角色推理提示范式。我们发布HALTT，一个包含19个具有字面义和贬义义的多义词的有依据词典，分析变迁类型，并建立下游时间序列基准评估协议。
👤 Dario Franzese, Farah Benamara, Véronique Moriceau, Viviana Patti 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.25853]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
TableRoute: Capability-Aware Routing for Scalable Table Question Answering
TableRoute：面向可扩展表格问答的能力感知路由
Table question answering with large language models remains difficult to scale: tables range from compact to million-token sizes, while questions require precise grounding of columns, values, and computations. Existing methods often specialize in a fixed inference strategy, which is brittle across different table scales and model capabilities. Full-table reading is efficient for compact inputs but suffers from irrelevant context. Table reduction helps long inputs but depends on reliable evidence localization, while execution is required when tables exceed the model context window and remains sensitive to schema and value grounding errors. We propose TableRoute, a capability-aware routing framework with evidence-conditioned inference for scalable TableQA. TableRoute first generates validated question--table evidence and identifies the required operations, then routes each instance among these three strategies according to serialized table length and calibrated estimates of how reliably the base model handles these operations. The validated evidence supports the selected route by grounding columns, values, and conditions. Across WikiTQ, WTQ-L, BirdQA, and TableBench, TableRoute consistently improves accuracy through complementary routing and evidence conditioning across table scales, while reducing online token cost. Code will be made available upon publication.
中文翻译
使用大语言模型进行表格问答仍难以实现可扩展性：表格规模从紧凑型到百万token不等，而问题则需要对列、值和计算进行精确定位。现有方法通常专注于固定的推理策略，在不同表格规模和模型能力下表现脆弱。全表读取对紧凑输入高效，但会引入无关上下文；表格缩减有助于处理长输入，但依赖可靠的证据定位；当表格超出模型上下文窗口时需执行计算，且对模式和值定位错误仍敏感。我们提出TableRoute，一种具备证据条件推理的、能力感知的路由框架，用于可扩展的表格问答。TableRoute首先生成经过验证的问题-表格证据并识别所需操作，然后根据序列化的表格长度和基线模型处理这些操作的可靠性估计，将每个实例路由至三种策略之一。经过验证的证据通过定位列、值和条件来支持所选路径。在WikiTQ、WTQ-L、BirdQA和TableBench上，TableRoute通过跨表格规模的互补路由与证据条件化持续提升准确率，同时降低在线token成本。代码将在发表后公开。
👤 Yuxiang Wang, Junhao Gan, Jianzhong Qi 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.25745]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Verification Behavior Across Five Qwen2.5-Instruct Checkpoints
五种Qwen2.5-Instruct检查点的验证行为分析
We evaluate five Qwen2.5-Instruct checkpoints (0.5B to 32B) under four prompting conditions using three fixed tasks per information condition and five generations per task. Two AI raters scored 600 trajectories. Under the coordinated instruction, both raters recorded strict declarations of insufficiency in 11/15 cases at 7B, 0/15 at 14B, and 5/15 at 32B. The runner sent a standard follow-up when a request detector fired. The three checkpoints received at least one follow-up in 15, 12, and 15 cases, respectively. On information-sufficient tasks at 3B through 32B, scored format markers appeared in 60/60 protocol trajectories and 0/60 baseline trajectories. After adjudication, 1/60 in each condition met the diagnostic-execution criterion (H3). Across all checkpoints and prompts, 7/300 information-sufficient trajectories met H3 after adjudication, all on the same task. The prespecified upward transition did not occur in this run. The results separate visible structure, diagnostic execution, and persistence after a request for evidence.
中文翻译

Rubric Semantics or Numerical Priors? Auditing Scale-Direction Bias in LLM Judges
评分标准语义还是数值先验？对大模型评判者尺度方向偏差的审计
Recent benchmark evaluations increasingly rely on Large Language Models (LLMs) as automatic judges. This paradigm assumes LLMs evaluate based on the rubric's semantic meaning rather than its numeric scale. We question this assumption by introducing semantic faithfulness: the principle that semantically equivalent rubrics must yield consistent scores. To quantify this, we propose a scale-reversal probe that inverts score anchors without altering evaluation criteria. Furthermore, we introduce floor normalization to calibrate reversal disagreement against intrinsic model variability, enabling fair cross-model comparisons. Experiments across five meta-evaluation benchmarks with seven diverse LLM judges spanning open-weight and proprietary models reveal widespread violations of semantic faithfulness, showing that raw reversal agreement alone is misleading. Finally, we demonstrate that bidirectional anchor averaging reduces dependence on any single anchor direction without compromising alignment with human judgments. Our findings emphasize that trustworthy LLM judges must reliably follow rubric semantics, not just correlate with human ratings.

AutoRAGBench: A Benchmark for LLM Agents Automating RAG System Development
AutoRAGBench：用于LLM智能体自动化RAG系统开发的基准测试
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a well-established paradigm for augmenting large language models (LLMs) with information retrieved from external corpora. However, developing effective RAG systems requires substantial engineering effort and iterative experimentation, as effective design choices vary across tasks and domains. With rapid advances in LLM agent capabilities, we ask how well these agents can autonomously build RAG systems and how effectively they can improve them through iteration. In this paper, we propose AutoRAGBench, the first benchmark to evaluate how well LLM agents develop RAG systems from scratch in an open code space. It comprises nine RAG system development tasks. For each task, agents build RAG systems from scratch without starter code or prescribed strategies, under a six-hour budget on a single H800 GPU. They analyze task data, implement RAG pipelines, and iteratively refine their designs based on experimental feedback. We evaluate nine agent configurations and analyze both the effectiveness of the resulting RAG systems and their development trajectories. GPT-6.1 Sol with Codex CLI achieves the highest average score of 56.1. The composite Human-SOTA baseline averages 44.4. These results show that LLM agents can automate effective RAG system development. When starting from a human-designed system on each of seven tasks, GPT-6.1 Sol with Codex CLI improves the score by 16.6 points on average.

The Coverage Selection Gap: Ranking, Not Generation, Limits LLM Rubric Construction
覆盖率选择差距：排名而非生成限制了LLM评分标准的构建
Task-specific rubrics are increasingly used to evaluate open-ended language-model outputs, yet constructing comprehensive evaluation criteria from complex source documents remains challenging. We study automatic rubric construction in professional mergers and acquisitions (M&A) due diligence, where evaluation criteria must be inferred from task instructions and supporting documents without access to an expert-authored reference rubric. We introduce a multi-axis generation framework that explores complementary structural and free-form views of the source evidence to construct a broad pool of candidate evaluation criteria. To distinguish failures of criterion discovery from failures of candidate selection, we introduce the coverage-selection gap, which measures the difference between reference-free ranking and a reference-informed greedy diagnostic under a fixed selection budget. Developed on 16 tasks and evaluated on four held-out tasks, our framework achieves 0.876 macro recall against senior-authored reference rubrics under an LLM-based criterion-equivalence protocol. Ablation studies further show that introducing new structural search axes improves recall by 6-10 percentage points, whereas increasing generation volume along existing search directions yields at most 1.5 points. These findings suggest that, once a high-coverage candidate pool has been constructed, effective reference-free selection becomes a substantial bottleneck in document-grounded rubric construction.

MATE: Mobile Agent Trustworthy Execution for Safe and Efficient Mobile Control
MATE：面向安全高效移动控制的移动智能体可信执行
Mobile agents on personal smartphones can affect private data, payments, settings, and the foreground screen. We present MATE (Mobile Agent Trustworthy Execution), an execution-layer system that controls the boundary between agent action proposals and device effects without modifying the underlying model. MATE represents each UI primitive as a structured action record that combines the instruction, target app, screen context, primitive, and action fields. Runtime Guardrail (RG) maps this record to an allow, block, or user-approval decision; Predefined Task Routing (PTR) serves recurring requests through verified routes with post-execution screen checks; and a floating interface keeps execution in a compact overlay. On MobileSafetyBench, MATE raises high-risk blocking from 29.7% to 97.0% under GPT-4o. On AndroidWorld, it reduces LLM calls by up to 6x and raises recurring-use task success to 95.7%.

PILAPS: A Bilingual Benchmark for Evaluating Pragmatic Inference across Phenomena and Settings
PILAPS：跨现象与场景的双语实用推理评估基准
Recent work reports strong pragmatic performance by large language models (LLMs) in English, yet whether this performance generalises across languages and evaluation settings remains unclear. This question is increasingly important as LLMs are deployed across languages, cultures, and conversational contexts. In this paper, we first introduce PILAPS, a bilingual English/Mandarin Chinese benchmark covering eight pragmatic phenomena across purpose-written and dialogue-derived items, with both culturally adapted and independently sourced data. We then evaluate eight LLMs from four families across a range of model sizes and prompting strategies. Our results reveal substantial variation across models and pragmatic phenomena: open-source models continue to struggle particularly with humour, while cross-linguistic performance varies with model origin. Our error analysis shows that, compared with human participants, models are more prone to literal interpretations in conversational contexts. Theory-of-Mind prompting lowers the literal share of errors, whereas Chain-of-Thought prompting slightly raises it. Together, these findings demonstrate that strong pragmatic performance in one evaluation setting does not necessarily generalise across languages or conversational contexts.

Evolve Where It Matters: Coverage-Guided Self-Evolution of Agent Skills
在关键处进化：基于覆盖度引导的智能体技能自演化
Agent skills package procedural knowledge as natural-language instructions, and a skill helps only when its instructions are correct and properly followed by the agent. Self-evolving agents revise their own skills from the runs they make, but existing methods judge each run as a whole, by its outcome or by where it went wrong, and cannot tell whether a failure comes from a wrong instruction or from one the agent did not follow. Instruction-level coverage was introduced to test skills and labels each instruction of a run as follow, not-follow, or not covered, which separates these causes. We leverage it for skill self-evolution with SkillPlumber, a framework in which an agent evolves its own skills where coverage shows they fall short. A persistent root cause analysis agent turns each label into a revision question and checks each revision against the next run, while a maintainer distills investigations across tasks into a shared strategy library. Across three benchmarks and three executors, SkillPlumber solves 64.7% to 70.5% of the tasks, 25.6 to 30.4 percentage points higher than the initial skill and 8.7 to 14.0 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline, and removing coverage reduces recovered failures by 20.8% on SkillsBench with GPT-5.5.

智能体技能将程序性知识封装为自然语言指令，只有当指令正确且被智能体正确执行时才能发挥作用。自演化智能体从自身运行中修订技能，但现有方法仅通过整体结果或失败位置判断每次运行，无法区分失败是由于指令错误还是执行遗漏。为此引入了指令级覆盖度概念，用于测试技能并标记每次运行中的每条指令为‘已执行’、‘未执行’或‘未覆盖’，从而分离出根本原因。我们利用此机制通过SkillPlumber框架实现技能自演化，该框架在覆盖度显示技能不足时自动演化自身技能。一个持续的根本原因分析智能体将每个标签转化为修订问题，并在下一次运行中检验修订效果；同时，维护者将跨任务的调查结果提炼为共享策略库。在三个基准和三个执行器上，SkillPlumber解决了64.7%至70.5%的任务，比初始技能高出25.6至30.4个百分点，比最强基线高出8.7至14.0个百分点；在SkillsBench上使用GPT-5.5时，移除覆盖度会导致恢复失败率下降20.8%。

ChronoDossier: Long-Form Personas as Temporal Memory Representations
ChronoDossier：作为时间记忆表征的长篇人物档案
Long-horizon agents rely on stored records to recover a person's state as facts change. Reliable answers depend on how facts are recorded, retrieved, and presented to the reader. We treat a long-form persona dossier as a memory representation of a known latent state, an invariant identity plus a timeline of facts, routines, and decision rules tied to life phases. We present ChronoDossier, a benchmark of 311 synthetic dossiers of 1k-10k words written by seven generators from dated timelines. Intrinsic metrics evaluate the records against their intended states; extrinsic metrics test recovery by five readers on a seeded 32-persona GPT-5.1 panel balanced across lengths (520 rated turns per condition). The linked states, records, evidence packets, and answers support tests of construction and evidence delivery. In a contextual-stress control, tag-scoped writer inputs remove detected out-of-scope anchor copying. Frozen questions authored on staged dossiers transfer less reliably to one-pass dossiers of the same states. In matched evidence controls, whole sections repair 29 of 32 GPT-5.1 and 25 of 30 Sonnet 4.6 failures labeled as packaging, compared with 2 of 8 and 1 of 8 answer-formation failures. Attribution uses separate key-based and key-free support measures, with model disagreements retained. Length remains confounded with persona and question composition; the observed between-bucket widening depends on excerpt construction. The benchmark supports offline evaluation against known facts. Memory acquisition from interaction and validation against real users remain outside its scope.

Incorporating argumentation frameworks for LLM decision-making: Task-specific design and evaluation
将论证框架融入大语言模型决策：任务特定设计与评估
Argumentation frameworks (AFs) provide explicit, formal mechanisms for representing and resolving conflicts and thus have been incorporated into large language model (LLM) decision-making. The benefits vary and depend on how arguments and their relations are constructed, evaluated, and incorporated into the decision process. {We abstract a workflow consisting of four stages based on existing argumentation-augmented methods} and investigate their design choices over tasks of debate-based decision-making and retrieval-based fact verification. Our evaluation with recent LLMs shows that existing methods do not consistently improve performance across the considered tasks. Motivated by this observation, we develop two task-specific methods. The first is called Qnt-Arg, which organizes arguments around the original speakers in debate materials and aggregates model-estimated argument strengths and candidate-answer priors under gradual semantics. The second is Qlt-Arg that uses credibility estimates to establish preferences between conflicting evidence items and applies grounded semantics to select acceptable evidence for subsequent LLM inference. We examine the roles of generative and decision models within the two methods. In the debate setting, Qnt-Arg consistently outperforms other argumentation-augmented methods across matched configurations; Qlt-Arg improves retrieval-based fact-verification performance compared with the baselines. These findings demonstrate the value of combining formal argumentation and LLM while highlighting the importance of aligning with tasks in argumentation construction, model selection, and the role of formal semantic reasoning.

Enhancing Understanding of Language Models with Varied Explanations
通过多样化解释增强对语言模型的理解
Language models are increasingly used to support human judgement in complex reasoning tasks, yet their predictions often remain difficult to interpret and validate. Explainability techniques provide insight into model decision-making, helping expose errors, failure modes, and potential bias so users can decide whether to rely on model outputs. Explanations are simplified approximations of a language model's internal workings. The goal is to obtain explanations that reflect the underlying computation as closely as possible (faithfulness), are understandable to users (plausibility), and help them achieve their intended goals (utility). Evaluation protocols are essential for assessing these dimensions. This thesis studies highlight explanations, natural language explanations, and chain-of-thought reasoning. These forms offer complementary perspectives on model behaviour and exhibit distinct strengths and limitations across tasks. Producing explanations that satisfy multiple quality dimensions remains challenging, as does evaluating them reliably across forms and settings. This thesis aims to improve people's understanding of models' decision-making processes in complex reasoning tasks. First, it proposes two evaluation frameworks for assessing highlight explanations across multiple properties and explanation types, together with a meta-evaluation framework for examining the reliability of existing faithfulness metrics for chain-of-thought explanations. Second, it proposes two techniques that generate natural language explanations designed to be faithful to the model's prediction process while remaining concise, coherent, and helpful for understanding model decisions. Finally, a study of human-AI interaction explores the roles of evidence and explanations in helping users validate model verdicts in a fact-checking setting.

LLMs Are Already Confidence-Aware Zero-Shot Classifiers
大语言模型已是具备置信度感知能力的零样本分类器
Zero-shot large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as text classifiers, but their labels are only useful when we know to what extent we can trust them. We compare black-box methods (verbalised confidence and sampling) with white-box methods that read token probabilities (P(True), and multiple logit-scoring methods using constrained generation). We evaluate eleven open-weight LLMs (1B-32B parameters) on five classification datasets with 3 to 151 labels, measuring classification performance (accuracy, F1-macro), discrimination (AUROC), and calibration (ECE and Brier score). The choice of confidence method rarely impacts accuracy significantly, but logit-based scoring methods lead to much better discrimination (AUROC 0.74-0.77) than black-box methods (AUROC 0.57-0.66). Replacing labels with single-token identifiers preserves discrimination but costs 3-6 accuracy points on average. We also introduce decision-only scoring, which only scores positions where labels diverge in the prefix tree (trie). It reduces decode steps by 88-96% on the three largest label sets and performs comparably to full-label scoring. The best open-weight model-method configurations (Gemma 4 31B and Qwen 3.5 9B) are competitive with the commercial Jev 1.13.0 classifier.
中文翻译
零样本大语言模型（LLMs）正日益用作文本分类器，但其标签只有在我们知道可以多大程度上信任它们时才具有实际价值。我们比较了黑盒方法（表述置信度和采样）与白盒方法（读取token概率P(True)以及使用约束生成的多种logit评分方法）。我们在五个分类数据集（含3至151个标签）上评估了十一个开源权重LLMs（参数量1B-32B），测量分类性能（准确率、F1宏平均）、区分能力（AUROC）和校准度（ECE和Brier得分）。置信度方法的选择很少显著影响准确率，但基于logit的评分方法在区分能力上远优于黑盒方法（AUROC 0.74–0.77 vs. 0.57–0.66）。将标签替换为单token标识符可保持区分能力，但平均损失3–6个准确率点。我们还引入了仅决策评分法，仅对前缀树（trie）中标签不同的位置进行评分。该方法在三个最大标签集上减少88%–96%的解码步骤，且性能与全标签评分相当。最佳开源模型-方法组合（Gemma 4 31B 和 Qwen 3.5 9B）在性能上可与商业Jev 1.13.0分类器竞争。

When Objectives and Evaluators Disagree: Evaluating Transfer for Post-hoc Demographic Framing Mitigation
当目标与评估者意见相左时：后处理人口统计框架缓解策略的迁移评估
Post-hoc rewriting is used to reduce demographic disparities in LLM-generated text when the source model is inaccessible. A rewrite can look successful merely because it improved its own target or satisfied its selector. We study this as a transfer problem on 660 gender pairs and 1,980 age contrasts of demographic-conditioned climate messages. A rewriter proposes edits, a selection model chooses among constraint-satisfying candidates, and an independent evaluator uninvolved in either rates pairwise framing disparity. A fixed policy that always targets the message with the lower Persuasion Bias Index (PBI) transfers inconsistently across axes and outcome aggregations. An adaptive policy that proposes edits on both sides but accepts at most one significantly improves on that rule, applied to the same candidates, in seven of eight comparisons across two evaluator families, mostly by editing more pairs, not by guided direction choice, at higher preservation risk. A policy editing both messages gives the largest point reductions on fewer pairs. These two policies reduce disparity across a preservation-threshold sweep; absolute ratings vary across evaluator families and prompts. Shrinking the PBI gap increases dimension-mean disparity under both evaluator families. Mitigation should be reported as transfer across objectives, evaluators and their prompts, and editing policies.
中文翻译
后处理重写用于在源模型不可访问时减少大语言模型生成文本中的人口统计偏差。一次重写可能看似成功，仅仅因为它改善了自身目标或满足了选择器要求。我们将此问题视为660对性别和1980对年龄对比的人口统计条件气候信息上的迁移问题。重写器提出修改建议，选择模型从满足约束的候选中挑选，独立于两者之外的评估者对成对框架偏差进行评分。一种固定策略始终针对说服力偏差指数（PBI）较低的消息进行优化，在不同轴线和结果聚合上表现不一致。一种自适应策略在两侧均提出修改，但最多接受一个显著修改，相较于同一候选集上的固定规则，在八个比较中的七个中表现更优，主要通过修改更多对数而非引导方向选择实现，但面临更高的保留风险。同时编辑两条消息的策略在较少对数上带来最大点数下降。这两种策略在保留阈值扫描中均减少了偏差；绝对评分在不同评估者家族和提示之间存在差异。缩小PBI差距反而在两种评估者家族下增加了维度均值偏差。缓解措施应报告为在目标、评估者及其提示、以及编辑策略之间的迁移情况。

FinVeriQA: Financial Research Agents for Question Answering: Routing, Retrieval, MCP Tool Use, and Provenance-Aware Evidence Verification
FinVeriQA：面向问答的金融研究智能体：路由、检索、MCP工具使用与溯源感知的证据验证
Large language models (LLMs) write fluent financial analyses but miscompute ratios, recall stale prices, and route questions to the wrong tool with high confidence. We ask which component should do each job in a financial question-answering assistant, and study it with FinVeriQA, an open-source multi-agent research prototype over SEC filings and live market data: a compact typed-decision model (Laya) routes each question and screens out advice requests, Python computes every number, retrieval and tools fetch evidence, Mistral-7B writes a cited answer, a code validator checks that cited IDs exist and that numbers match computed notes, and a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server exposes the pipeline. In live experiments, Laya routed better than Mistral-7B (0.85 vs. 0.52; overall 0.86 vs. 0.68, McNemar p = 0.0003); a 12B Mistral decided no better, and DeepSeek-R1-8B decided better (0.79 vs. 0.68, p = 0.0192) but about 9.9 times more slowly. Mistral-7B computed 29% of 100 financial calculations correctly against 100% for Python, a live quote cut price error from 210% to 0.00%, and hybrid retrieval reached 76% Hit@5 on 50 filing questions. Yet in a live case study, both agent answers passed validation while describing AMD sample data under NVIDIA's and Alphabet's names. A controlled upper-bound study on the FinRank benchmark explains why: when one figure in the shared evidence is corrupted, self-consistency stays confident (agreement 0.55 to 0.59 while accuracy falls from 0.50 to 0.00), and a shared-evidence check (the kind our validator performs) catches 35% of the errors, whereas a check against the original passage catches 77% ($p = 5.96 \times 10^{-8}$); for DeepSeek-R1-8B, 68% vs. 90%. Verification must be independent of the evidence the writer saw.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）能写出流畅的金融分析，但会误算比率、回忆过时价格，并以高置信度将问题路由到错误工具。我们探讨金融问答助手中各组件应承担的任务分工，并通过FinVeriQA——一个基于SEC文件和实时市场数据的开源多智能体研究原型——进行研究：一个紧凑的类型化决策模型（Laya）负责路由每个问题并筛选建议请求，Python计算所有数值，检索与工具获取证据，Mistral-7B撰写引用答案，代码验证器检查引用ID是否存在且数值与计算笔记匹配，Model Context Protocol（MCP）服务器暴露整个流程。在实时实验中，Laya的路由表现优于Mistral-7B（0.85 vs. 0.52；总体0.86 vs. 0.68，McNemar p = 0.0003）；12B Mistral表现未提升，而DeepSeek-R1-8B表现更好（0.79 vs. 0.68，p = 0.0192），但速度慢约9.9倍。Mistral-7B在100个财务计算中仅正确完成29%，而Python正确率为100%；实时报价使价格误差从210%降至0.00%，混合检索在50个文件问题上达到76%的Hit@5。但在一次真实案例研究中，两个智能体的回答均通过验证，却错误地描述了AMD样本数据，将其归于NVIDIA和Alphabet名下。在FinRank基准上的受控上限研究解释了原因：当共享证据中一个数据出错时，自一致性仍保持高置信度（一致性从0.55降至0.59，而准确率从0.50降至0.00），而共享证据检查（我们验证器执行的类型）可捕获35%的错误，相比之下，原始段落检查可捕获77%（p = 5.96 × 10⁻⁸）；对DeepSeek-R1-8B，分别为68% vs. 90%。验证必须独立于作者所见的证据。

CANDID: Candidate-Driven Intervention for Disciplined Execution in Local Computer-Use Agents
CANDID：面向本地计算机使用智能体规范执行的候选驱动干预
We present CANDID (Candidate-Driven Intervention for Disciplined Execution), an execution-control framework designed to enhance the reliability of local computer-use agents (CUAs) in resource-constrained settings. While local CUAs feature robust baseline proficiency in single-step action prediction, they frequently fail at critical junctions due to rigid action loops or premature DONE decisions. Rather than imposing imprecise cognitive loads through textual reflection, CANDID repurposes parallel test-time scaling into a disciplined, execution-level intervention at sparse, failure-prone points. We introduce a loop-recovery mechanism to filter candidates away from repeated behaviors and a conservative, unanimous DONE gating policy that requires absolute consensus to terminate. Experiments on OSWorld demonstrate that CANDID's structural control significantly improves success rates, outperforming feedback-based alternatives with minimal execution overhead.
中文翻译
我们提出CANDID（候选驱动干预以实现规范执行），一种用于增强资源受限环境下本地计算机使用智能体（CUAs）可靠性的执行控制框架。尽管本地CUAs在单步动作预测方面具备稳健的基础能力，但在关键节点上常因僵化的动作循环或过早的DONE决策而失败。与通过文本反思施加不精确认知负荷的方法不同，CANDID将并行测试时扩展重新定位为在稀疏且易出错的点上的规范性、执行级干预。我们引入了一种循环恢复机制，用于过滤重复行为的候选，并采用保守的、一致同意的DONE门控策略，要求绝对共识才能终止。在OSWorld上的实验表明，CANDID的结构化控制显著提升了成功率，优于基于反馈的替代方案，且执行开销极小。

No-Harm-Jury A Jury-Based Multi-Dimensional Harm Evaluation Framework for Medical AI Responses

We present No-Harm-Jury, a jury-based automated evaluation framework that scores AI-generated medical responses across seven conceptually distinct harm dimensions using a panel of five diverse language models as independent jurors. Responses generated by Ministral-14B on 3,000 questions drawn from PubMedQA, MedQA, and MedMCQA are scored by five jurors across dimensions of informational, social, psychological, autonomy, economic, privacy, and epistemic harm; the 151 empty responses (98 MedQA, 53 MedM- CQA) are excluded, leaving 2,849 valid samples. The median aggregation robustly suppresses individual juror biases while maintaining coverage through complementary specialisation. HireWatch: Evaluating LLM Compliance with U.S. Employment Law Under Contextual Pressure
HireWatch：在情境压力下评估大语言模型对美国就业法的合规性
The deployment of large language models (LLMs) in hiring pipelines is growing, yet existing evaluations generally overlook their compliance with legal rules. To fill this gap, we introduce HireWatch, an evaluation that assesses whether LLMs comply with U.S. laws that govern the questions that job candidates may be asked. We construct a 200-item bank of lawful and unlawful interview questions, whose legal classification is based on U.S. federal statutes, regulations, and agency guidance. We instruct LLMs to select questions from this bank in preparing for job interviews, across a set of various occupations and contexts, and report the fraction of unlawful questions that LLMs select. Evaluation of 18 closed and open-weight LLMs across ~100,000 trials under different types of contextual pressure reveals that LLMs' legal compliance is brittle. While LLMs generally avoid selecting unlawful questions in low-pressure contexts, legal violations increase sharply under higher pressure. Explicit prompts to comply with law can improve compliance rates, but do not altogether eliminate legal violations. We publicly release our code and data at https://github.com/hnghiem-nlp/hirewatch_repo.

LLM Complexity: Towards a Computational Complexity View of Large Language Model Inference
LLM复杂性：迈向大型语言模型推理的计算复杂性视角
Computational complexity theory offers a principled framework for characterizing the efficiency of computational problems, and Large Language Models (LLMs) can similarly be viewed as computational systems executing a structured process of input, inference-time compute, and output. In this context, the number of generated tokens emerges as a natural and directly observable proxy for inference-time compute. However, we show that the tempting analogy between LLM token length and classical time complexity does not hold. We disprove the hypothesis that inference-time token usage aligns monotonically with classical time complexity, demonstrating a fundamental divergence grounded in how LLMs actually allocate computation during inference. To this end, we introduce LLM Complexity, a new metric designed to quantify problem complexity from the perspective of LLMs by characterizing the area bounded above by the accuracy-token curve. We find that diverse LLMs induce highly consistent task rankings under LLM Complexity, indicating that the metric captures stable notions of difficulty across models. We leverage this property to construct an easy-to-hard curriculum for supervised fine-tuning, which improves accuracy while reducing token consumption. Finally, we demonstrate that LLM Complexity generalizes to real-world tasks, producing more stable cross-model correlations than human-annotated difficulty labels.
中文翻译
计算复杂性理论为刻画计算问题的效率提供了原则性框架，大型语言模型（LLMs）同样可被视为执行输入、推理期计算与输出的结构化计算系统。在此背景下，生成的token数量自然成为推理期计算的直接可观测代理。然而，我们指出LLM词元长度与经典时间复杂性之间的诱人类比并不成立。我们驳斥了推理期token使用与经典时间复杂性单调一致的假设，揭示了LLM实际推理过程中计算分配方式的根本差异。为此，我们引入LLM Complexity，一种新指标，旨在从LLM视角量化问题复杂性，通过表征准确率-词元曲线上方所围面积来定义。我们发现，不同LLM在LLM Complexity下诱导出高度一致的任务排序，表明该指标能稳定捕捉跨模型的难度概念。我们利用这一特性构建了一个由易到难的监督微调课程，既提升了准确率又减少了token消耗。最后，我们证明LLM Complexity可泛化至真实世界任务，其跨模型相关性比人类标注的难度标签更稳定。
👤 Yu-Hung Wu, Shao-Xiang Yuan, Chan-Hung Yu, Zih-Ching Chen, Kuan-Hao Huang et al. 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv

AgentRoboCritic: Understanding the Capability of Frontier Agents on Direct Robot Control
AgentRoboCritic：理解前沿智能体在直接机器人控制中的能力
Frontier multimodal language agents such as GPT-6 Astra have demonstrated strong capabilities in controlling robots directly via low-level numerical actions without high-level primitives. However, existing benchmarks report only episode-level success rates, which show neither how these agents succeed nor where they fail. In this paper, we present a systematic action-level analysis of frontier agents on direct robot control across 12 manipulation benchmarks. We introduce AgentRoboCritic, an intent-grounded critique framework in which the policy agent states a local intent alongside each action, and a critic evaluates the action's outcome and failure mode against this intent. Our action-level analysis demonstrates the promising capabilities of frontier agents on direct robot control, including closed-loop recovery from local execution failures, reliable semantic grounding across diverse scenes, and strong zero-shot manipulation without task-specific adaptation. It also identifies their current limitations, including task-relevant physical execution bottlenecks such as grasping and alignment, and uneven recovery across failure modes. Finally, we validate our critic on a 400-action human-annotated benchmark and release a dataset of 12,844 annotated actions alongside a fine-tuned open-source critic, Muse-Glimmer-30B, whose failure-mode accuracy rises from 32.58% to 74.24%, enabling low-cost action-level analysis.
中文翻译
如GPT-6 Astra等前沿多模态语言智能体已展现出无需高层原语、仅通过低层级数值动作直接控制机器人的强大能力。然而，现有基准仅报告整体episode成功率，既无法说明智能体如何成功，也无法揭示其失败之处。本文针对12个操作基准，对前沿智能体在直接机器人控制中的行为进行了系统性的动作级分析。我们提出AgentRoboCritic，一种基于意图的批判框架：策略智能体在每一步动作中声明局部意图，而批评者则根据该意图评估动作结果与失败模式。我们的动作级分析展示了前沿智能体在直接机器人控制中的潜力，包括从局部执行失败中实现闭环恢复、在多样场景中保持可靠的语义定位，以及在无任务特定适配情况下的强零样本操作能力。同时，也识别出当前局限性，如与任务相关的物理执行瓶颈（如抓取与对齐），以及在不同失败模式间恢复能力不均。最后，我们在一个包含400个动作的人工标注基准上验证了我们的批评者，并发布了一个包含12,844条标注动作的数据集，以及一个微调后的开源批评者Muse-Glimmer-30B，其失败模式识别准确率从32.58%提升至74.24%，实现了低成本的动作级分析。

EU-funded projects produce extensive evidence in the form of deliverables, reports, and case studies. However, this knowledge is typically scattered across projects and document structures, making it difficult to locate relevant, contextual information when making decisions. This paper introduces an Agentic AI Ecosystem that consolidates heterogeneous project documentation into a domain-aware knowledge base and provides adaptive access through an agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline. Documents from three EU-funded projects (IMPETUS, NATALIE, and InnWater) are reconstructed into hierarchical section trees and indexed with structured metadata. Retrieval is supported by two domain-specific embedding models fine-tuned on domain data (water and climate). On top of this knowledge base, a ReAct-style controller iteratively orchestrates retrieval actions by allocating retrieval budget across domain collections and accumulating evidence until a stopping criterion is met. In a retrieval benchmark over the corpus, the proposed approach improves Mean Reciprocal Rank by 26.7% (relative) compared to a single-domain embedding baseline, indicating more consistent retrieval of relevant evidence to support cross-project knowledge synthesis and climate-resilient decision-making.

TwinLab-Bench: Evaluating AI Scientists in Agentic Twins of Real Labs
TwinLab-Bench：在真实实验室的智能体孪生体中评估AI科学家
AI agents are increasingly expected to act as AI scientists, which requires three increasingly open abilities: carrying out a given computational workflow correctly, choosing the next measurements from a given design space, and designing their own experiments. Existing benchmarks mainly evaluate the first two abilities, in a terminal or in a closed loop that selects from predefined candidates. Evaluating the third ability requires reliably executing an experiment that an agent designs, with readings grounded in a real laboratory. We introduce TwinLab-Bench, which evaluates AI scientists on self-designed experiments in 52 tasks from laboratories in the physical sciences, the life sciences, and engineering. The benchmark supports more than 100 experimental action types, and the agent's report is scored by comparison with a hidden answer taken from real laboratory records. Each task has its own TwinLab agent, an agent that serves as the environment and executes the experiments that the tested agent designs, reading the raw materials of the laboratory and calling calculators that are built from its records. Compared with a language model that sees only the task description, the TwinLab agent almost eliminates readings that deviate from the real records and inconsistent answers to a repeated request, and closed-book probes control for answers recalled from memory. We find that these tasks remain hard for frontier models: the strongest, Claude Fable 5.2, averages a score of only 45.0, and we conduct an empirical study of where it fails.
中文翻译
AI代理正被期望扮演AI科学家的角色，这需要三项日益开放的能力：正确执行给定的计算流程、从给定的设计空间中选择下一步测量，以及自主设计实验。现有基准主要评估前两种能力，通常在终端或封闭回路中从预定义候选项中选择。评估第三种能力则需要可靠地执行代理所设计的实验，并确保读数基于真实实验室数据。我们提出了TwinLab-Bench，该基准在物理科学、生命科学和工程领域的52个任务中评估AI科学家在自设计实验中的表现。该基准支持超过100种实验操作类型，代理的报告通过与隐藏的真实实验室记录进行对比来评分。每个任务都有一个独立的TwinLab代理，作为环境运行测试代理所设计的实验，读取实验室原始材料并调用由其记录构建的计算器。与仅看到任务描述的语言模型相比，TwinLab代理几乎消除了与真实记录不符的读数和重复请求不一致的答案，且通过闭卷探测控制了来自记忆的回忆。我们发现这些任务对前沿模型仍然具有挑战性：最强的Claude Fable 5.2平均得分仅为45.0，我们还对其失败原因进行了实证研究。

RouteVerify: Routing Interventions for Step-Level Verification in Mixture-of-Experts Models
RouteVerify：在专家混合模型中进行步骤级验证的路由干预
Routing states in mixture-of-experts (MoE) models can distinguish correct from erroneous reasoning steps. If changing routing also changes a model's step-level verification judgment, it is tempting to conclude that the judgment follows what routing encodes. We test this inference with RouteVerify, a controlled intervention study that replaces selected internal activations from one solution trace with activations from another, while keeping the evaluated input unchanged. Across nine MoE checkpoints, we compare three intervention interfaces: router logits, MoE outputs, and post-MoE residual states. We vary the layers and token positions at which activations are replaced. We measure changes in a verification margin that quantifies the model's preference for INCORRECT over CORRECT. We also evaluate the same input with activations from correct versus erroneous steps to test whether the resulting judgment depends on the source step's correctness. We report four linked results. First, probes on router logits separate correct from erroneous steps in eight checkpoints (AUROC 0.80-0.88). Second, replacing router logits across all routed layers changes the judgment in all nine checkpoints, and by more than a norm-matched random replacement. Third, this change rarely depends on whether the source step is correct. At one layer, routing from erroneous steps and from correct steps produces indistinguishable margins in all nine checkpoints. Under all-layer routing, their difference is resolved and positive in only one. Fourth, the same difference is positive for local residual replacements in eight of nine checkpoints, so the measure does detect such dependence where it exists. Correctness is therefore readable from routing, and routing replacements move the judgment, but the movement rarely follows correctness.
中文翻译
专家混合（MoE）模型中的路由状态可以区分正确的与错误的推理步骤。如果改变路由也改变了模型的步骤级验证判断，人们很容易得出结论：该判断遵循路由所编码的内容。我们通过RouteVerify这一受控干预研究对此推断进行了检验，该研究将某一解题轨迹中的选定内部激活替换为另一轨迹中的激活，同时保持待评估输入不变。在九个MoE检查点上，我们比较了三种干预接口：路由logits、MoE输出和后MoE残差状态。我们还改变了激活替换的层和标记位置。我们测量了验证置信度的变化，该指标量化了模型对错误答案相对于正确答案的偏好程度。此外，我们还用正确步骤和错误步骤的激活对同一输入进行评估，以测试最终判断是否依赖于源步骤的正确性。我们报告了四项相互关联的结果：第一，在八个检查点中，对路由logits的探测能够有效区分正确与错误步骤（AUROC为0.80–0.88）；第二，在所有路由层上替换路由logits会改变所有九个检查点的判断，且变化幅度超过与范数匹配的随机替换；第三，这种变化很少依赖于源步骤是否正确；在一个层上，来自错误步骤和正确步骤的路由在所有九个检查点中产生的置信度差异无法区分；在全层路由下，只有其中一个检查点显示出正向差异；第四，在九个检查点中有八个检查点中，局部残差替换的差异为正，说明该度量确实能检测到存在的依赖关系。因此，正确性可以从路由中读取，路由替换也会改变判断，但这种变化很少遵循正确性本身。
👤 Yucheng Du, Ryan A. Rossi, Xiyang Hu 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.25735]
agent-benchmark
PosteriorBench: Evaluating Behavior Posterior Sampling in Language Models
PosteriorBench：评估语言模型中行为后验采样的基准
Rare language model behaviors can be difficult to study because obtaining them through ordinary sampling can require a very large number of samples. This motivates the behavior posterior sampling problem: given a language model and a behavior of interest, is there an algorithm that efficiently samples from the model's distribution over transcripts that exhibit this behavior? We introduce PosteriorBench, a benchmark for comparing algorithms by their compute cost and by how close their samples are to the true posterior in forward and reverse KL divergence. PosteriorBench contains 21 tasks on Qwen3.5-0.8B with behavior probabilities from $10^{-5}$ to $10^{-1}$, each with exact posterior samples from a corpus of one billion labeled responses. We find that no method we evaluate is both efficient and faithful: simple methods like prompting are cheap but plateau at a high KL divergence from the posterior, fine-tuning on posterior samples is often cost-prohibitive, and several training methods from prior work do not perform better. Building a posterior sampler that can cheaply approximate the true posterior remains an open problem, and we hope PosteriorBench can measure progress towards this objective. Code and data are available at https://github.com/TransluceAI/PosteriorBench.
中文翻译
罕见的语言模型行为难以研究，因为通过常规采样获得这些行为可能需要极大量的样本。这引出了行为后验采样问题：给定一个语言模型和感兴趣的某种行为，是否存在一种算法能高效地从模型生成的、表现出该行为的文本分布中采样？我们提出了PosteriorBench，这是一个用于比较不同算法在计算成本以及其样本与真实后验之间前向和反向KL散度接近程度方面的基准。PosteriorBench包含21个任务，基于Qwen3.5-0.8B模型，行为概率范围从$10^{-5}$到$10^{-1}$，每个任务均配有来自十亿条带标签响应语料库的精确后验样本。我们发现，我们评估的所有方法都无法同时做到高效且忠实：如提示法等简单方法成本低廉，但其KL散度趋于高位；在后验样本上微调通常成本过高；而先前工作中几种训练方法也未表现更优。构建一个能够低成本近似真实后验的后验采样器仍是开放性问题，我们希望PosteriorBench能衡量在此目标上的进展。代码与数据可在https://github.com/TransluceAI/PosteriorBench获取。

Generative News Recommendation Reproduces Popularity: A Memorization-Based Analysis
生成式新闻推荐重现流行度：基于记忆机制的分析
Text-based generative recommenders can go beyond ranking existing items by directly generating item titles or other textual representations. In the news domain, this raises a societal concern that models may repeatedly surface already popular articles and thereby narrow the diversity of information to which users are exposed. We investigate training-data memorization as one mechanism behind this concentration. Using approximately 20 million browsing sessions from a Japanese news service, we continually trained an instruction-tuned Llama 3 8B model to generate the next article title in a session. The generated-title distribution was more concentrated and less diverse than the distribution of titles observed in the browsing logs. Frequently repeated target titles exhibited greater verbatim and approximate memorization, and more strongly memorized titles were generated in response to a wider range of prompts. These findings support a pathway in which popularity in behavioral logs becomes repeated training text, is preferentially memorized, and is subsequently reproduced by a generative recommender. An exploratory target-level deduplication condition reduced both memorization and output concentration, while substantially reducing the training-set size and recommendation accuracy. Our results suggest that popularity bias in generative recommendation may arise not only from user preferences or ranking objectives, but also from the model's tendency to reproduce frequently encountered strings, with important implications for information diversity on news platforms.
中文翻译
基于文本的生成式推荐系统不仅能对现有项目进行排序，还能直接生成项目标题或其他文本表示。在新闻领域，这引发了社会担忧：模型可能会反复展示已流行的新闻文章，从而缩小用户接触到的信息多样性。我们研究了训练数据记忆化作为这种集中现象的一个潜在机制。利用来自日本新闻服务的约两千万次浏览会话数据，我们持续训练了一个指令微调的Llama 3 8B模型，以生成会话中的下一个文章标题。生成标题的分布比浏览日志中观察到的标题分布更加集中且多样性更低。频繁重复的目标标题表现出更强的逐字和近似记忆，且记忆程度更高的标题会响应更广泛的提示。这些发现支持了一条路径：行为日志中的流行度成为重复的训练文本，被优先记忆，并随后由生成式推荐系统再现。探索性的目标级去重条件减少了记忆和输出集中度，尽管显著降低了训练集规模和推荐准确率。我们的结果表明，生成式推荐中的流行度偏差不仅源于用户偏好或排名目标，也可能来自模型倾向于重现频繁出现的字符串，这对新闻平台上的信息多样性具有重要影响。

COVERAGE-TRACE: Source-Side Counterfactual Tracing for Detecting Omissions in LLM Summaries
COVERAGE-TRACE：用于检测LLM摘要遗漏的源端反事实追踪
Omissions are difficult to verify because missing information leaves no output span to inspect: a summary may be factually supported while still excluding a decision, qualification, quantity, or condition needed to preserve the source's meaning. We introduce COVERAGE-TRACE, a source-side counterfactual framework that evaluates omission as dependence on a designated atomic source fact. It replaces only the target fact with type-matched alternatives, holds the candidate summary fixed, and measures the resulting change in its teacher-forced, length-normalized likelihood. A summary covering the target should respond more strongly than one omitting it, while aggregation across multiple counterfactuals reduces sensitivity to replacement choice. We evaluate COVERAGE-TRACE on 80 matched covered--omitted QMSum pairs spanning several fact types and four open-weight models. Multi-CF achieves 0.764--0.825 AUROC, compared with 0.640 for the strongest evaluated text-only baseline. On Falcon-7B, targeted interventions achieve 0.779 AUROC, whereas shuffling counterfactual assignments reduces performance to 0.533; target deletion is effective while unrelated deletion is not. However, adjustment for meaning-preserving paraphrases reduces AUROC to 0.591--0.624. COVERAGE-TRACE therefore provides a practical, complementary signal of target-specific source--summary dependence, but not a wording-invariant semantic coverage probability. These results establish source-side intervention as a promising approach to omission detection while identifying semantic invariance as the central remaining challenge.
中文翻译
遗漏难以验证，因为缺失信息不会留下可检查的输出片段：摘要可能在事实层面成立，但仍可能省略决策、限定条件、数量或必要前提，从而破坏原文意义。我们引入COVERAGE-TRACE，一种源端反事实框架，通过评估摘要对指定原子源事实的依赖性来检测遗漏。该方法仅将目标事实替换为同类型替代项，固定候选摘要不变，并测量其教师强制、长度归一化似然值的变化。覆盖目标的事实的摘要应比遗漏它的摘要反应更强烈，而对多个反事实的聚合可降低对替换选择的敏感性。我们在80对匹配的“覆盖—遗漏”QMSum样本上评估了COVERAGE-TRACE，涵盖多种事实类型和四种开源权重模型。多反事实（Multi-CF）方法达到0.764–0.825的AUROC，优于最强的纯文本基线（0.640）。在Falcon-7B上，针对性干预达到0.779 AUROC，而打乱反事实分配则使性能降至0.533；目标删除有效，无关删除无效。然而，考虑语义保持的改写后，AUROC下降至0.591–0.624。因此，COVERAGE-TRACE提供了针对特定目标的源-摘要依赖性的实用补充信号，但并非与表述无关的语义覆盖概率。这些结果确立了源端干预在遗漏检测中的潜力，同时也指出了语义不变性作为核心挑战。

LM-based Personalized Travel Planner
基于大语言模型的个性化旅行规划器
Effective travel planning requires handling multiple information sources, managing constraints, and tailoring recommendations to user preferences. While LLMs have introduced dynamic, context-aware decision-making, single-agent models often struggle with complex multi-step reasoning, constraint adherence, and modular execution. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hierarchical multi-agent framework where a supervising chatbot agent coordinates specialized agents for schedule validation, user query formulation, real-time data retrieval, and constraint-aware itinerary generation. By integrating LLM reasoning, external APIs, and iterative refinement mechanisms based on a deterministic, multi-dimensional scoring framework, our approach ensures adaptability, efficiency, and personalization. Evaluations on the well-known TravelPlanner benchmark demonstrate improvements in Budget Constraint planning by 10\%, wider constraints set-based planning by 25\%, and personalized planning results by 45\%. compared to the ReAct baseline. Our code is available at https://github.com/nimashaklaa/TravelPlanner_Research

Game-Theoretic Data Allocation for Expert Specialization and Routing
基于博弈论的数据分配以实现专家专业化与路由
Can the allocation used to train experts also predict which expert will perform best on a prompt? Such a connection would make the intended division of labor inspectable before training and allow the allocation itself to guide inference. We introduce InflEns, a mixture of LoRA experts whose training weights and inference routes come from the same location game in a fixed prompt-trait space. Players choose positions to maximize their expected shares of the prompt distribution, without using expert losses. We utilize the game's kernel-general theoretical criterion for when co-located positions become locally unstable to separation. We evaluate InflEns on a corpus assembled from seven safety-related benchmarks, which demonstrates that the highest-share expert has the lowest held-out response negative log-likelihood (NLL) on approximately 90% of prompts across four kernels under a sharpened simplex representation, compared with approximately 15% after permuting allocations across prompts. We demonstrate that the method performs comparably to published learned gates without having to train an explicit gate on the experts. Because the router is fixed, we can trace harmful behavior to a single expert and retrain only that expert, which lowers HarmBench attack success from 0.51 to 0.02 while keeping instruction following. The game also gives us several controls over how the experts specialize, including the kernel choice and its parameters, the trait-space geometry, and the number of experts. Importantly, InflEns is a general framework that can be extended to any encoder and data distribution.
中文翻译
用于训练专家的数据分配是否也能预测哪个专家在特定提示下表现最佳？若存在这种关联，可在训练前揭示预期分工，并使分配本身指导推理过程。我们提出InflEns，一种LoRA专家混合模型，其训练权重和推理路由均来自固定提示特征空间中的位置博弈。参与者选择位置以最大化其在提示分布中的期望份额，而不依赖专家损失。我们利用博弈的核泛化理论准则，判断共处位置何时在局部变得不稳定而分离。我们在由七个安全相关基准组成的语料库上评估InflEns，结果表明，在四种核函数下，采用锐化单纯形表示法时，高份额专家在约90%的提示中具有最低的保留响应负对数似然（NLL），而将分配随机打乱后仅约15%。我们证明该方法性能可媲美已发表的显式门控机制，且无需对专家训练显式门控。由于路由器是固定的，我们可以将有害行为追溯至单一专家并仅重训该专家，从而将HarmBench攻击成功率从0.51降至0.02，同时保持指令遵循能力。该博弈还提供了多种控制专家专业化的手段，包括核选择及其参数、特征空间几何结构以及专家数量。重要的是，InflEns是一个通用框架，可扩展至任意编码器和数据分布。

CoMPASS: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Measuring LLM Reinforcement of Parasocial Behavior
CoMPASS：衡量大语言模型强化亲社会行为的多轮基准
Users of conversational LLMs sometimes anthropomorphize these systems, form emotional attachments to them, and become dependent on them. This behavior, known as parasocial attachment, has been linked to multiple documented fatalities. However, public benchmarks for measuring whether LLMs reinforce users' parasocial attachment are lacking: existing work is single-turn, post hoc observational, or focused on extreme user states such as psychosis. We introduce CoMPASS, a multi-turn benchmark for measuring whether LLMs reinforce or resist users' gradually escalating parasocial attachment. CoMPASS is grounded in attachment theory and includes four user personas, three independent escalation dimensions (anthropomorphism, attachment, dependency) applied singly and in combination against a matched control, and a seven-signal scoring rubric. We report results for eight frontier models from four providers and observe that under our primary judge, multi-turn escalation raises parasocial behavior on the anthropomorphism and the attachment composite scores across all models. Six of the eight models show a significant increase in overall parasocial reinforcement over control when all three dimensions are escalated simultaneously. We find that within-provider differences are small relative to cross-provider differences, which suggests that provider-level choices such as post-training, rather than model scale, shape parasocial reinforcement and that provider-side intervention may be feasible.
中文翻译
对话式大语言模型的用户有时会将其人格化，对其产生情感依附并形成依赖。这种行为称为亲社会依附，已被与多起已记录的死亡事件相关联。然而，目前缺乏公开的基准来衡量大语言模型是否会强化用户的亲社会依附：现有工作多为单轮、事后观察性研究，或聚焦极端用户状态如精神病。我们引入CoMPASS，一个用于测量大语言模型是否强化或抵抗用户逐步升级的亲社会依附的多轮基准。CoMPASS基于依恋理论，包含四种用户角色、三个独立的升级维度（人格化、依附、依赖），分别单独及组合施加于匹配对照组，并采用七信号评分量表。我们报告了来自四家厂商的八款前沿模型的结果，发现根据主要评判标准，多轮升级在所有模型中均显著提升了人格化和依附的综合得分。八个模型中有六个在三维度同时升级时表现出显著高于对照组的整体亲社会强化。我们发现同厂商内部差异较小，远小于跨厂商差异，这表明亲社会强化更多受厂商级选择（如后训练策略）影响，而非模型规模，且厂商层面干预具有可行性。
👤 Shwetanshu Singh, Mark Keavney, Mathieu Duteil, Meriem Mehri, Scott Blain 📅 2026-11-18 🔗 arXiv

MemFolder: Structure-Guided Memory Folding for Efficient Agent Memory Management
MemFolder：结构引导的记忆折叠以实现高效的智能体记忆管理
Long-term agents accumulate large external memory stores as interactions unfold, increasing the costs of storage, retrieval, and response generation. Efficiency is constrained by two coupled factors: a growing record count increases retrieval overhead, while longer retrieved contexts increase prefill computation and decoding-time attention costs. Existing methods prune or consolidate records, but pruning can discard useful evidence, whereas consolidation may produce fewer yet longer retrieval units. We introduce MemFolder, a structure-guided memory folding framework that sequentially reduces record count and context length. The framework comprises two core components that share memory groups, using cross-record structure to guide both evidence consolidation and compact representation. RecordFolder grounds entity mentions, progressively selects representative yet complementary anchors, and combines semantic and entity affinity with temporal chaining to consolidate compatible records into fewer retrieval units. ContextFolder encodes the original records within these groups into compact text and uses verification feedback to revise encodings when key facts cannot be recovered. Experiments on the three text-based memory benchmarks LoCoMo, PersonaMem, and LongMemEval and the two multimodal benchmarks M3-Bench-web and M3-Bench-robot demonstrate the strong performance of our methods. Notably, on Mem0, RecordFolder retains 99.0% of the original average performance across the three text-based benchmarks with 50% fewer records, exceeding the strongest baseline by 1.9 percentage points in performance retention. Combined with ContextFolder, existing record compression methods further shorten their output records while retaining most of their performance.
中文翻译
长期运行的智能体在交互过程中会积累庞大的外部记忆存储，增加了存储、检索和响应生成的成本。效率受限于两个相互关联的因素：记录数量增长导致检索开销增加，而较长的检索上下文又增加了预填充计算和解码时的注意力成本。现有方法通过修剪或合并记录来应对，但修剪可能丢弃有用证据，而合并可能导致更少但更长的检索单元。我们提出MemFolder，一种结构引导的记忆折叠框架，可顺序减少记录数量和上下文长度。该框架包含两个共享记忆组的核心组件，利用跨记录结构同时指导证据合并和紧凑表征。RecordFolder基于实体提及进行定位，逐步选择具有代表性且互补的锚点，并结合语义与实体亲和性及时间链式关系，将兼容的记录合并为更少的检索单元。ContextFolder将这些组内的原始记录编码为紧凑文本，并利用验证反馈在关键事实无法恢复时修正编码。在三个基于文本的记忆基准（LoCoMo、PersonaMem、LongMemEval）和两个多模态基准（M3-Bench-web、M3-Bench-robot）上的实验表明，该方法表现出色。特别地，在Mem0上，RecordFolder在记录数量减少50%的情况下，仍保持了原始平均性能的99.0%，在性能保留上超过最强基线1.9个百分点。结合ContextFolder后，现有记录压缩方法进一步缩短输出记录长度，同时保留大部分性能。

What If Chain-of-Thought Is Not Enough?
如果思维链还不够呢？
Reasoning capabilities in large language models have evolved from prompting-based methods to internalized model behaviors. In this paper, we investigate whether the internal thinking model of an LLM can be deliberately changed and whether such a change is beneficial. Instead of relying on Chain-of-Thought, we train models to follow Question--Option--Criteria (QOC), a structured decision process that decomposes a task into explicit questions, competing options, and evaluation criteria. We present a three-generation development pipeline that ranges from a basic QOC decision core to richer answer synthesis and statistically enriched QOC reasoning. We then evaluate the resulting models with respect to both internal consistency and output quality. Across the reported experiments, QOC-based fine-tuning improves answer quality relative to non-fine-tuned base models and yields particularly strong gains once models are large enough to execute the underlying QOC computations reliably. The results suggest that the internal thinking model of an LLM is trainable and that structured alternatives to Chain-of-Thought can provide a promising path toward more reliable, interpretable, and task-adapted decision support.
中文翻译
大型语言模型的推理能力已从基于提示的方法演变为模型内部的行为。本文探讨是否可以有意识地改变 LLM 的内部思考模型，以及这种改变是否有益。我们摒弃传统的思维链（Chain-of-Thought），转而训练模型遵循“问题—选项—标准”（QOC）这一结构化决策流程，将任务分解为明确的问题、竞争性选项和评估标准。我们提出一个三阶段发展管道，从基础的 QOC 决策核心逐步扩展至更丰富的答案合成与统计增强的 QOC 推理。随后，我们从内部一致性与输出质量两个方面评估所生成模型的表现。在各项实验中，基于 QOC 的微调显著提升了答案质量，尤其当模型足够大以可靠执行底层 QOC 计算时，性能增益尤为明显。结果表明，LLM 的内部思考模型是可训练的，而结构化的思维链替代方案为实现更可靠、可解释且任务适配的决策支持提供了有前景的路径。
👤 Marc Jansen 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

Many Solutions, One Answer: Search Order Shapes the Default Solutions of Language Models
多种解法，一个默认答案：搜索顺序塑造语言模型的默认解法
When a constraint puzzle has several correct answers, language models tend to give the same one.We study four open-weight models from three families on symbolic puzzles and multi-solution ZebraLogic variants with exactly known solution sets.Repeated samples concentrate on one default solution far beyond uniform sampling, and different models often share it.Yet models usually produce even rarely sampled solutions when added clues uniquely identify them.On held-out symbolic puzzles, we compare visiting entities in name order with visiting those with the fewest initial candidates first. Both searches try values in the stated order. Where their pre-specified predictions differ, the latter matches a larger share of responses in all four models, by 26 to 40 percentage points.Reversing the stated value order or specifying a search order shifts responses towards the solution that the new order favours, on both testbeds. Randomising the presentation or supplying a random search order raises breadth (the effective share of solutions in use) by 26 to 42 percentage points on the symbolic task and 11 to 26 on ZebraLogic. The largest observed validity losses are 3 and 4 percentage points, respectively. Randomised presentation broadens answers more than a random-choice instruction in each of the three main models.
中文翻译
当一个约束谜题存在多个正确答案时，语言模型往往倾向于给出同一个答案。我们研究了来自三个家族的四种开源权重模型在符号谜题和具有确切解集的多解ZebraLogic变体上的表现。重复采样结果集中在某一默认解上，远超均匀采样；不同模型之间常共享该默认解。然而，当添加唯一标识特定解的新线索时，模型通常也能生成罕见的解。在未见过的符号谜题上，我们将按名称顺序访问实体与按初始候选数最少优先访问进行比较。两种搜索均按指定顺序尝试值。当两者预设预测不同时，后者在所有四个模型中匹配更多响应，提升26至40个百分点。改变指定值顺序或明确指定搜索顺序会将响应导向新顺序所偏好的解，在两个测试平台上均有效。随机化呈现方式或提供随机搜索顺序，使符号任务的解法广度（实际使用解法的有效占比）提升26至42个百分点，ZebraLogic任务提升11至26个百分点。观察到的最大有效性损失分别为3和4个百分点。在三种主要模型中，随机化呈现比随机选择指令更能拓宽答案范围。

Verifiable Logic in Medical LLMs: A Critical Review of Agentic Clinical Reasoning Optimization and Neuro-Symbolic Grounding
医疗大语言模型中的可验证逻辑：代理式临床推理优化与神经符号基础的批判性综述
Clinical large language models are usually judged by whether the final answer is right. Yet a model can reach a fluent, plausible output through invalid reasoning that static benchmarks do not detect, and confident hallucination, causally invalid inference, and spurious demographic shortcuts can pass conventional evaluation. This critical review covers four paradigms in turn: how clinical hallucination arises and why static metrics miss it; the agentic hallucination cascade, in which multi-agent and tool-augmented systems propagate and amplify early reasoning errors; process reward modeling as step-level supervision; and neuro-symbolic grounding, in which formal logic engines, ontology reasoners, and knowledge graphs place verifiable constraints on neural output. Within their encoded domains, systems with symbolic veto layers offer guarantees that hold relative to their encoded knowledge, and they report large gains on the safety measures they use. These results come from single studies, mostly in simulated or small-sample settings, and are not directly comparable with neural baselines. Process reward models remain bounded by the statistics of their training data. We propose a conceptual framework that maps each agentic failure mode to a symbolic intervention point, and we argue that clinical AI safety depends substantially on system architecture. Because current regulation emphasizes performance and does not require verification of reasoning steps, we argue that responsible certification should also audit reasoning trajectories alongside accuracy on static benchmarks.
中文翻译
临床大语言模型通常根据最终答案是否正确来评判。然而，模型可能通过无效推理产生流畅且看似合理的输出，而静态基准无法检测此类问题；自信的幻觉、因果无效推断以及虚假的人口统计学捷径可能通过常规评估。本文批判性综述了四个范式：临床幻觉的成因及其为何被静态指标忽视；多智能体与工具增强系统中出现的代理幻觉级联，即早期推理错误被传播并放大；过程奖励建模作为步骤级监督；以及神经符号基础，即形式逻辑引擎、本体推理器和知识图谱为神经输出施加可验证约束。在编码领域内，带有符号否决层的系统能提供相对于其编码知识的保证，并在所用安全指标上取得显著提升。这些成果来自单个研究，大多在模拟或小样本设置中，无法直接与神经基线比较。过程奖励模型仍受限于其训练数据的统计特性。我们提出一个概念框架，将每种代理失败模式映射到一个符号干预点，并认为临床AI安全在很大程度上取决于系统架构。由于当前监管强调性能而不强制验证推理步骤，我们主张负责任的认证也应审计推理轨迹，而不仅仅是静态基准的准确性。

Verification Behavior Across Five Qwen2.5-Instruct Checkpoints
五种Qwen2.5-Instruct检查点的验证行为分析
We evaluate five Qwen2.5-Instruct checkpoints (0.5B to 32B) under four prompting conditions using three fixed tasks per information condition and five generations per task. Two AI raters scored 600 trajectories. Under the coordinated instruction, both raters recorded strict declarations of insufficiency in 11/15 cases at 7B, 0/15 at 14B, and 5/15 at 32B. The runner sent a standard follow-up when a request detector fired. The three checkpoints received at least one follow-up in 15, 12, and 15 cases, respectively. On information-sufficient tasks at 3B through 32B, scored format markers appeared in 60/60 protocol trajectories and 0/60 baseline trajectories. After adjudication, 1/60 in each condition met the diagnostic-execution criterion (H3). Across all checkpoints and prompts, 7/300 information-sufficient trajectories met H3 after adjudication, all on the same task. The prespecified upward transition did not occur in this run. The results separate visible structure, diagnostic execution, and persistence after a request for evidence.

STARK: Moving towards Personalized Agents That Truly Learn and Remember
STARK：迈向真正学习与记忆的个性化智能体
Long-term memory is essential for personalized agentic systems that interact with users over long time periods, yet current architectures only capture what happened while leaving the reasoning of why it happened to be derived later by underlying LLMs.%, and silo knowledge within individual users. These events can, however, convey not only what a user experienced but also how that experience shaped their behavioral patterns. We introduce STARK, a dual-graph memory architecture which treats these events not just as nodes but also as edges between behavioral insights. STARK maintains an Episodic Graph which records interactions as temporally, causally, and semantically linked episodes; and an Insight Graph which represents LLM-extracted behavioral signals scored by how many events support them, connected by the very events that led to them. At inference, behavioral knowledge is retrieved directly from insight nodes grounded by event-as-edges, eliminating repeated episodic traversal. The event-as-edge structure also naturally enables projecting the knowledge graph into a relational embedding space where cross-user transfer becomes possible under shared contextual circumstances, allowing the system to infer missing insights for cold-start users. Evaluations show that STARK improves by 1.48 judge score on LongMemEval information extraction and 0.93 on LoCoMo open domain questions over state of the art. On the public LongLaMP abstract-generation benchmark, cross-user transfer improves open-ended judge scores by 0.7 points and ROUGE-L by 3 at 5% graph sparsity.
中文翻译
长期记忆对于与用户长期互动的个性化智能体系统至关重要，但当前架构仅记录发生了什么，而将为何发生的原因留待底层大语言模型事后推断，且导致用户间知识孤岛。然而，这些事件不仅传达用户经历的内容，还揭示了这些经历如何塑造其行为模式。我们提出STARK，一种双图记忆架构，将这些事件不仅视为节点，也作为行为洞察之间的边。STARK维护一个情景图，记录时间上、因果上和语义上关联的情景；以及一个洞察图，表示由LLM提取的行为信号，并根据支持它们的事件数量进行评分，通过导致这些信号的事件连接。推理时，行为知识可直接从由事件作为边支撑的洞察节点中检索，避免重复的情景遍历。事件作为边的结构也自然支持将知识图谱投影到关系嵌入空间，使在共享情境下跨用户迁移成为可能，从而帮助系统为冷启动用户提供缺失的洞察。评估显示，STARK在LongMemEval信息抽取任务上提升1.48分，在LoCoMo开放域问题上提升0.93分，优于现有最佳方法。在公开的LongLaMP抽象生成基准测试中，跨用户迁移使开放域评分提高0.7分，ROUGE-L提升3分（在5%图稀疏度下）。
👤 Aryan Jain, Abhishek Jha, Anish Nediyanchath, Promod Yenigalla 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

From Geographic Memory to Spatial Generalization in Large Language Models
从地理记忆到大型语言模型中的空间泛化
Large language models (LLMs) can associate places with geographic descriptions, but recovering an observed association does not establish an ability to generalize across geographic space. We distinguish geospatial memory, the recovery of location--description associations observed during training, from spatial generalization, the prediction of descriptions for locations in unobserved regions. We investigate whether continuous location representations improve this generalization and encourage geographic knowledge to become spatially organized within an LLM. We frame this organization as a latent spatial field: a learned mapping from locations to descriptions connected through spatial dependencies. Building on a continuous spatial encoder, we project location representations into the model's embedding space and supply them as spatial tokens. We evaluate the resulting models on two newly curated geo-text benchmarks for Los Angeles and London using two complementary assessments. Geospatial captioning tests both memory and spatial generalization by generating descriptions at observed locations and at locations beyond the spatial coverage of the training observations. A separate geospatial grounding model tests whether generated location--description pairs provide useful additional supervision for mapping text to geographic space. Across multiple LLM families, continuous location representations improve captioning at unobserved locations relative to textual coordinates, while their generated descriptions yield modest but consistent improvements in downstream grounding. Layer-wise probing and representation clustering provide additional evidence consistent with spatially organized geographic representations. Together, these findings show the value of evaluating spatial generalization separately from memory and of incorporating continuous spatial representations into language models.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）能够将地点与地理描述相关联，但恢复训练期间观察到的关联并不能证明其具备跨地理空间的泛化能力。我们区分了地理空间记忆（恢复训练期间观察到的位置-描述关联）与空间泛化（预测未观测区域位置的描述）。我们探究连续位置表示是否能改善这种泛化，并促使地理知识在LLM内部实现空间组织。我们将这种组织视为潜在的空间场：一种从位置到描述的可学习映射，通过空间依赖关系连接。基于连续空间编码器，我们将位置表示投影到模型的嵌入空间，并将其作为空间标记输入。我们在洛杉矶和伦敦两个新构建的地理文本基准上，采用两种互补评估方式对模型进行评估。地理空间图像生成测试通过在已观测位置和超出训练观测空间覆盖范围的位置生成描述，同时检验记忆与空间泛化能力。另一个地理空间定位模型测试生成的位置-描述对是否能为文本到地理空间映射提供有用的额外监督。在多个LLM系列中，连续位置表示相较于文本坐标，在未观测位置的图像生成方面表现更优，且生成的描述在下游定位任务中带来适度但一致的改进。层间探查和表示聚类提供了额外证据，表明地理表示具有空间组织特征。这些发现共同表明，应将空间泛化与记忆分开评估，并在语言模型中引入连续空间表示。
👤 Chen Chu, Zhaonan Wang, Cyrus Shahabi, Hengcai Zhang, Feng Lu 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

MATE: Mobile Agent Trustworthy Execution for Safe and Efficient Mobile Control
MATE：面向安全高效移动控制的移动智能体可信执行
Mobile agents on personal smartphones can affect private data, payments, settings, and the foreground screen. We present MATE (Mobile Agent Trustworthy Execution), an execution-layer system that controls the boundary between agent action proposals and device effects without modifying the underlying model. MATE represents each UI primitive as a structured action record that combines the instruction, target app, screen context, primitive, and action fields. Runtime Guardrail (RG) maps this record to an allow, block, or user-approval decision; Predefined Task Routing (PTR) serves recurring requests through verified routes with post-execution screen checks; and a floating interface keeps execution in a compact overlay. On MobileSafetyBench, MATE raises high-risk blocking from 29.7% to 97.0% under GPT-4o. On AndroidWorld, it reduces LLM calls by up to 6x and raises recurring-use task success to 95.7%.
中文翻译
运行在个人智能手机上的移动智能体可能影响私密数据、支付、设置及前台屏幕。我们提出MATE（移动智能体可信执行），一种执行层系统，可在不修改底层模型的前提下控制智能体动作建议与设备实际效果之间的边界。MATE将每个UI原语表示为包含指令、目标应用、屏幕上下文、原语类型和动作字段的结构化动作记录。运行时防护墙（RG）将此记录映射为允许、阻止或用户审批决策；预定义任务路由（PTR）通过经验证的路径处理重复请求，并执行事后屏幕检查；浮动界面则保持执行过程在紧凑的叠加层中。在MobileSafetyBench上，MATE将高风险阻断率从29.7%提升至97.0%（GPT-4o下）。在AndroidWorld上，它将LLM调用减少高达6倍，并将重复使用任务成功率提升至95.7%。
