CANDID: Candidate-Driven Intervention for Disciplined Execution in Local Computer-Use Agents
CANDID：面向本地计算机使用智能体规范执行的候选驱动干预
We present CANDID (Candidate-Driven Intervention for Disciplined Execution), an execution-control framework designed to enhance the reliability of local computer-use agents (CUAs) in resource-constrained settings. While local CUAs feature robust baseline proficiency in single-step action prediction, they frequently fail at critical junctions due to rigid action loops or premature DONE decisions. Rather than imposing imprecise cognitive loads through textual reflection, CANDID repurposes parallel test-time scaling into a disciplined, execution-level intervention at sparse, failure-prone points. We introduce a loop-recovery mechanism to filter candidates away from repeated behaviors and a conservative, unanimous DONE gating policy that requires absolute consensus to terminate. Experiments on OSWorld demonstrate that CANDID's structural control significantly improves success rates, outperforming feedback-based alternatives with minimal execution overhead.
中文翻译
我们提出CANDID（候选驱动干预以实现规范执行），一种用于增强资源受限环境下本地计算机使用智能体（CUAs）可靠性的执行控制框架。尽管本地CUAs在单步动作预测方面具备稳健的基础能力，但在关键节点上常因僵化的动作循环或过早的DONE决策而失败。与通过文本反思施加不精确认知负荷的方法不同，CANDID将并行测试时扩展重新定位为在稀疏且易出错的点上的规范性、执行级干预。我们引入了一种循环恢复机制，用于过滤重复行为的候选，并采用保守的、一致同意的DONE门控策略，要求绝对共识才能终止。在OSWorld上的实验表明，CANDID的结构化控制显著提升了成功率，优于基于反馈的替代方案，且执行开销极小。
👤 Woongkyu Lee, Jungwook Choi 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.24971]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
The ROI of Small Examples: Optimizing In-Context Learning Under Token Budgets
小样本的投入产出比：在令牌预算下的上下文学习优化
In-context learning (ICL) improves large language model performance at inference time without updating model parameters. However, these gains are not free: in practical deployments, longer prompts directly increase inference cost and latency. Under a fixed token budget, prompt construction therefore becomes a resource-allocation problem. While prior work has often focused on how many examples to include in ICL, we study the complementary question of whether the available budget is better spent on a few long demonstrations or on a larger number of shorter ones. We study this aspect in question answering (QA), by organizing candidate demonstrations into length-based bins and constructing prompts from examples within the same length range under fixed budgets. Experiments on two QA benchmarks (\textit{Beat the AI} and \textit{Natural Questions}), using six instruction-tuned LLMs, show that shorter demonstrations are generally the most effective use of prompt space. This effect is especially strong on \textit{Beat the AI}, and remains visible on NQ, although gains are smaller and saturate earlier in the long-context setting. An additional natural language inference experiment on ANLI R3 finds that one of the two shorter bins performs best for five of six models, although longer demonstrations remain competitive. Overall, our results highlight demonstration granularity as an important factor in prompt design.
中文翻译

Global Importance Estimation for KV Cache Eviction
KV缓存淘汰的全局重要性估计
Memory consumption of the key-value (KV) cache remains a central bottleneck for long-context LLM inference. We introduce PRKV, a training-free KV eviction method that estimates multi-hop global token importance by applying Personalized PageRank over attention-induced transition graphs. This enables importance propagation beyond recency or instantaneous attention, without modifying the model. We benchmark PRKV on long-context understanding, sparse retrieval, and chain-of-thought reasoning tasks using multiple LLM families. Under moderate to aggressive compression ratios, including up to 90%, PRKV achieves competitive task performance against established eviction baselines while reducing peak memory usage with low end-to-end overhead. These results demonstrate that multi-hop global influence estimation yields a favorable accuracy--memory trade-off for scalable long-context inference.

Physics of Language Models: Part 4.2, Canon Layers at Scale where Synthetic Pretraining Resonates in Reality
语言模型的物理：第四部分第2节，大规模下的规范层——合成预训练在现实中产生共鸣
Do standard benchmarks see the architectural differences that matter? We study it with 64 from-scratch pretraining runs of Transformers and three linear-model families at 1.3-8B parameters on 1-2T Nemotron-CC tokens, at a cost of roughly 100k USD per 8B run. In academic-scale pretraining (1.3B parameters, 100B tokens), architectural differences can all but disappear when the models are carefully tuned. Instead, at 1-2T tokens they become measurable: Canon layers, which add local token mixing, consistently improve Transformers, saving about 30% of pretraining tokens for the same MMLU accuracy at 8B. We also introduce GLA5 and GDN2, which improve gated linear attention and Gated DeltaNet with low-rank output gates. Surprisingly, with Canon, the simplest linear model, gated linear attention, matches Gated DeltaNet and outperforms Mamba2 even at 8B/1T. Therefore, the extra machinery of Gated DeltaNet and Mamba2 may do little more than mix neighboring tokens. Even at 8B/1T, however, high scores on standard benchmarks can hide weaknesses in basic in-context skills. In particular, linear models that match or beat Transformers on these benchmarks cannot, in fact, reliably retrieve information from a short 500-token context. Moreover, no model, Transformer or linear, reliably beats random guessing on arguably the simplest two-hop question, stated in a few short sentences, even at this scale. These findings $\textbf{challenge traditional architecture research}$ based primarily on standard benchmark scores. In contrast, small synthetic playgrounds, which predicted the architectural differences above at <100M parameters, can reveal what these benchmarks miss. For practitioners, one lesson is to include in-context reasoning examples in pretraining data from an early stage, rather than reserve them for later mid-training, let alone post-training.
中文翻译
标准基准能否捕捉到重要的架构差异？我们通过在1.3-8B参数规模下对64次从头开始的Transformer预训练运行以及三种线性模型家族，在1-2T Nemotron-CC数据上进行了研究，每次8B模型的训练成本约为10万美元。在学术规模的预训练（13亿参数，1000亿token）中，当模型经过精细调优后，架构差异几乎消失；但在1-2T token规模下，这些差异变得可测量：添加局部token混合的规范层（Canon layers）持续提升了Transformer性能，在8B模型上实现相同MMLU准确率时可节省约30%的预训练token。我们还引入了GLA5和GDN2，分别改进了门控线性注意力和门控DeltaNet，采用低秩输出门。令人惊讶的是，使用规范层后，最简单的线性模型在8B/1T规模下，其门控线性注意力表现已与门控DeltaNet相当，甚至优于Mamba2。因此，门控DeltaNet和Mamba2的额外机制可能仅起到混合相邻token的作用。然而，即使在8B/1T规模下，标准基准上的高分也可能掩盖基本上下文内技能的缺陷。特别是，那些在基准上匹配或超越Transformer的线性模型，实际上无法可靠地从短至500 token的上下文中检索信息。此外，无论Transformer还是线性模型，在这一规模下都无法可靠地击败随机猜测来回答一个显然最简单的两跳问题（仅用几句话陈述）。这些发现挑战了主要依赖标准基准分数的传统架构研究。相比之下，小型合成实验环境（在<1亿参数时就预测了上述架构差异）能够揭示这些基准所遗漏的内容。对实践者的启示是：应尽早将上下文推理示例纳入预训练数据，而非留到中期甚至后期再处理。

ICARE: cross-domain text classification with incremental class-aware representation and distillation learning
ICARE：基于增量类感知表示与蒸馏学习的跨域文本分类
The rise of powerful, fine-tuned large language models (LLMs) necessitates robust natural language inference (NLI) systems. These NLI systems ideally train across various tasks, encompassing diverse concepts like sentiment analysis, news classification, and coreference resolution. However, this continual, multi-tasking approach can lead to catastrophic forgetting, where previously learned tasks are lost, especially in domains lacking specific labels. Rehearsing past examples with labels can further exacerbate this issue by causing overfitting, hindering the system's ability to classify new categories. To tackle these challenges, this research proposes ICARE, a novel framework with two key components: an instance selection module and a knowledge distillation method. The former is based on representation learning, which meticulously selects training examples to present to the NLI system. The latter, knowledge distillation with feature and logit-level transfer, facilitates knowledge transfer between tasks. This leverages pruning techniques and optimized objective functions to transfer both essential feature-level and decision-making (logit-level) knowledge, mitigating catastrophic forgetting while adapting to new patterns. Extensive evaluations on benchmark text classification datasets have demonstrated ICARE's effectiveness. The framework achieves a significant improvement in target accuracy and reduction in the ``forgetting metric''. These results solidify ICARE's ability to preserve and leverage learned knowledge across diverse tasks, ultimately enhancing the adaptability and performance of NLI systems. The source code is available at https://github.com/CongSon01/ICARE_NLP.git

强大且微调过的大型语言模型（LLMs）的兴起，要求具备鲁棒的自然语言推理（NLI）系统。理想的NLI系统应在多种任务间持续训练，涵盖情感分析、新闻分类、共指消解等多样化概念。然而，这种持续多任务方法可能导致灾难性遗忘，即先前学习的任务丢失，尤其在缺乏特定标签的领域中更为严重。通过标签回放过去样本会进一步加剧此问题，导致过拟合，阻碍系统对新类别的分类能力。为应对这些挑战，本研究提出ICARE，一种包含两个核心组件的新框架：实例选择模块和知识蒸馏方法。前者基于表示学习，精心挑选训练样本来呈现给NLI系统；后者采用特征级与logit级传递的知识蒸馏，促进任务间的知识迁移。该方法结合剪枝技术与优化目标函数，实现关键特征级与决策级（logit级）知识的有效转移，缓解灾难性遗忘并适应新模式。在多个基准文本分类数据集上的广泛评估表明，ICARE显著提升了目标准确率并降低了“遗忘指标”。这些结果巩固了ICARE在跨多样化任务中保留与利用已有知识的能力，最终增强了NLI系统的适应性与性能。源代码可在 https://github.com/CongSon01/ICARE_NLP.git 获取。

LLM Complexity: Towards a Computational Complexity View of Large Language Model Inference
LLM复杂性：迈向大型语言模型推理的计算复杂性视角
Computational complexity theory offers a principled framework for characterizing the efficiency of computational problems, and Large Language Models (LLMs) can similarly be viewed as computational systems executing a structured process of input, inference-time compute, and output. In this context, the number of generated tokens emerges as a natural and directly observable proxy for inference-time compute. However, we show that the tempting analogy between LLM token length and classical time complexity does not hold. We disprove the hypothesis that inference-time token usage aligns monotonically with classical time complexity, demonstrating a fundamental divergence grounded in how LLMs actually allocate computation during inference. To this end, we introduce LLM Complexity, a new metric designed to quantify problem complexity from the perspective of LLMs by characterizing the area bounded above by the accuracy-token curve. We find that diverse LLMs induce highly consistent task rankings under LLM Complexity, indicating that the metric captures stable notions of difficulty across models. We leverage this property to construct an easy-to-hard curriculum for supervised fine-tuning, which improves accuracy while reducing token consumption. Finally, we demonstrate that LLM Complexity generalizes to real-world tasks, producing more stable cross-model correlations than human-annotated difficulty labels.
中文翻译
计算复杂性理论为刻画计算问题的效率提供了原则性框架，大型语言模型（LLMs）同样可被视为执行输入、推理期计算与输出的结构化计算系统。在此背景下，生成的token数量自然成为推理期计算的直接可观测代理。然而，我们指出LLM词元长度与经典时间复杂性之间的诱人类比并不成立。我们驳斥了推理期token使用与经典时间复杂性单调一致的假设，揭示了LLM实际推理过程中计算分配方式的根本差异。为此，我们引入LLM Complexity，一种新指标，旨在从LLM视角量化问题复杂性，通过表征准确率-词元曲线上方所围面积来定义。我们发现，不同LLM在LLM Complexity下诱导出高度一致的任务排序，表明该指标能稳定捕捉跨模型的难度概念。我们利用这一特性构建了一个由易到难的监督微调课程，既提升了准确率又减少了token消耗。最后，我们证明LLM Complexity可泛化至真实世界任务，其跨模型相关性比人类标注的难度标签更稳定。

AgentRoboCritic: Understanding the Capability of Frontier Agents on Direct Robot Control
AgentRoboCritic：理解前沿智能体在直接机器人控制中的能力
Frontier multimodal language agents such as GPT-6 Astra have demonstrated strong capabilities in controlling robots directly via low-level numerical actions without high-level primitives. However, existing benchmarks report only episode-level success rates, which show neither how these agents succeed nor where they fail. In this paper, we present a systematic action-level analysis of frontier agents on direct robot control across 12 manipulation benchmarks. We introduce AgentRoboCritic, an intent-grounded critique framework in which the policy agent states a local intent alongside each action, and a critic evaluates the action's outcome and failure mode against this intent. Our action-level analysis demonstrates the promising capabilities of frontier agents on direct robot control, including closed-loop recovery from local execution failures, reliable semantic grounding across diverse scenes, and strong zero-shot manipulation without task-specific adaptation. It also identifies their current limitations, including task-relevant physical execution bottlenecks such as grasping and alignment, and uneven recovery across failure modes. Finally, we validate our critic on a 400-action human-annotated benchmark and release a dataset of 12,844 annotated actions alongside a fine-tuned open-source critic, Muse-Glimmer-30B, whose failure-mode accuracy rises from 32.58% to 74.24%, enabling low-cost action-level analysis.

GLiDE: A Fast, Calibrated Decision Model Distilled from Reasoning Teachers
GLiDE：从推理教师中蒸馏出的快速、校准决策模型
Decision models enable fast, confidence-aware decisions by scoring candidate answers in a single forward pass, without generating text or intermediate reasoning. Yet reasoning can substantially improve decision quality, raising the question of whether its benefits can be retained without the cost of generating it. We investigate this question with \textbf{GLiDE}, a family of decision models spanning 0.4B to 27B parameters. Building on Qwen hybrid language models, GLiDE enables bidirectional interactions between the context, question, and candidate answers by removing causal masking from full-attention layers. To transfer reasoning capabilities into single-pass inference, we combine soft-target distillation from multiple teachers, including a reasoning model, with progressive post-training on diverse decision tasks. We further explore parameter-free recurrent depth to increase inference-time computation without generating tokens. Across five model sizes, GLiDE lies on the performance--parameter Pareto frontier of the Hugging Face \DI{}, with each model outperforming evaluated alternatives of equal or smaller size. \dmtwo{} (27B) achieves a hidden-set score of 71.52, with an expected calibration error of 2.0\% and a median latency of 92.9\,ms on a single GPU. Further checkpoint averaging and parameter-free recurrent depth yield GLiDE~27B, which reaches 73.37 with an expected calibration error of 2.1\%.

Democratizing Cross-Project Knowledge for Transforming Water Governance and Climate Resilience: an Agentic AI Ecosystem

EU-funded projects produce extensive evidence in the form of deliverables, reports, and case studies. However, this knowledge is typically scattered across projects and document structures, making it difficult to locate relevant, contextual information when making decisions. This paper introduces an Agentic AI Ecosystem that consolidates heterogeneous project documentation into a domain-aware knowledge base and provides adaptive access through an agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline. Documents from three EU-funded projects (IMPETUS, NATALIE, and InnWater) are reconstructed into hierarchical section trees and indexed with structured metadata. HiSA: Sparsifying the Hidden Dimension of RoPE Attention in LLMs
HiSA：稀疏化LLM中RoPE注意力的隐藏维度
Attention in large language models (LLMs) scores every query against every key with a full dot product. We investigate whether this dot product must be computed completely for attention to be effective. We propose Hidden Sparse Attention (HiSA), which keeps the query and key projections intact but only computes a partial dot product, following a static, parameter-free rule set by the token positions: attention becomes sparse in the hidden (feature) dimension, an axis much less explored than token sparsity, rather than over tokens as in almost all sparse attention methods. Based on the structure of rotary position embeddings (RoPE), we decompose keys and queries into parts which can be omitted when computing attention. By training LLMs from scratch with HiSA, we find that this partial dot product does not hurt but improves performance compared with the complete product while computing only about a third of the query-key products. During inference HiSA improves decoding speed and shrinks memory footprint. We also extend HiSA to FlashAttention-3 kernels for training and inference. Finally, we motivate our approach by showing that dense attention learns to suppress the attention score parts that HiSA drops. Code is available at https://github.com/ambroise-b/hisa.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）中的注意力机制会对每个查询与每个键执行完整的点积运算。我们探究这种点积是否必须完全计算才能使注意力有效。我们提出了隐藏稀疏注意力（HiSA），它保持查询和键投影不变，但仅根据由标记位置决定的静态、无参数规则集计算部分点积：注意力在隐藏（特征）维度上变得稀疏，这一轴线远未被广泛探索，不同于几乎所有稀疏注意力方法所关注的标记稀疏性。基于旋转位置编码（RoPE）的结构，我们将键和查询分解为可省略的部分以计算注意力。通过从头训练使用HiSA的LLM，我们发现这种部分点积不仅不会损害性能，反而优于完整点积，同时仅计算约三分之一的查询-键乘积。在推理阶段，HiSA提升了解码速度并缩小了内存占用。我们还将HiSA扩展至FlashAttention-3内核，用于训练和推理。最后，我们通过展示密集注意力会学习抑制HiSA所舍弃的注意力分数部分，来论证我们方法的合理性。代码已公开于https://github.com/ambroise-b/hisa。
👤 Ambroise Borbely, Mathieu Salzmann, Olivier Verscheure 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

BADAS-3.0: Real-Time Driving Perception and Planning from One Camera
BADAS-3.0：基于单摄像头的实时驾驶感知与规划
We ask how far a driving model can get with one camera and real-time latency. BADAS-3.0 is a 1B-parameter video encoder with four small heads. From two seconds of one forward camera, it predicts the current speed, the metric path just driven, a collision-risk score and six candidate futures over 6.4 seconds, in under 50 ms. Given the car's current speed, BADAS-3.0 has 44% lower minADE$_6$ over five trajectory benchmarks than the 34B Alpamayo 2 Super, which reads six cameras and runs over 100x slower. The gain comes from camera rigs that neither model was trained on. On the rig they share, the two are on par. Existing benchmarks are mostly nominal driving, so we add two open-loop safety benchmarks, one of real dashcam collisions and near-misses and one of Euro NCAP scenarios. On both, BADAS-3.0 has a higher pass rate than every published planner we tested.
中文翻译
我们探讨了仅使用单个摄像头和实时延迟的驾驶模型能达到何种程度。BADAS-3.0是一个10亿参数的视频编码器，带有四个小型头部。仅需两秒的前向摄像头输入，它就能在50毫秒内预测当前车速、已行驶的度量路径、碰撞风险评分以及未来6.4秒内的六个候选轨迹。在五项轨迹基准测试中，相较于340亿参数的Alpamayo 2 Super（读取六个摄像头，运行速度慢100倍以上），BADAS-3.0的最小平均距离误差（minADE$_6$）降低了44%。这一优势来源于两个模型均未在其中训练过的摄像头装置。在共享装置上，两者表现相当。现有基准大多为名义驾驶场景，因此我们新增了两个开环安全基准：一个是真实行车记录仪中的碰撞与险情事件，另一个是欧洲新车评估计划（Euro NCAP）场景。在这两个基准上，BADAS-3.0的通过率均高于我们测试的所有已发表规划器。
👤 Roni Goldshmidt, Hamish Scott, Lorenzo Niccolini 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

TwinLab-Bench: Evaluating AI Scientists in Agentic Twins of Real Labs
TwinLab-Bench：在真实实验室的智能体孪生体中评估AI科学家
AI agents are increasingly expected to act as AI scientists, which requires three increasingly open abilities: carrying out a given computational workflow correctly, choosing the next measurements from a given design space, and designing their own experiments. Existing benchmarks mainly evaluate the first two abilities, in a terminal or in a closed loop that selects from predefined candidates. Evaluating the third ability requires reliably executing an experiment that an agent designs, with readings grounded in a real laboratory. We introduce TwinLab-Bench, which evaluates AI scientists on self-designed experiments in 52 tasks from laboratories in the physical sciences, the life sciences, and engineering. The benchmark supports more than 100 experimental action types, and the agent's report is scored by comparison with a hidden answer taken from real laboratory records. Each task has its own TwinLab agent, an agent that serves as the environment and executes the experiments that the tested agent designs, reading the raw materials of the laboratory and calling calculators that are built from its records. Compared with a language model that sees only the task description, the TwinLab agent almost eliminates readings that deviate from the real records and inconsistent answers to a repeated request, and closed-book probes control for answers recalled from memory. We find that these tasks remain hard for frontier models: the strongest, Claude Fable 5.2, averages a score of only 45.0, and we conduct an empirical study of where it fails.
中文翻译
AI代理正被期望扮演AI科学家的角色，这需要三项日益开放的能力：正确执行给定的计算流程、从给定的设计空间中选择下一步测量，以及自主设计实验。现有基准主要评估前两种能力，通常在终端或封闭回路中从预定义候选项中选择。评估第三种能力则需要可靠地执行代理所设计的实验，并确保读数基于真实实验室数据。我们提出了TwinLab-Bench，该基准在物理科学、生命科学和工程领域的52个任务中评估AI科学家在自设计实验中的表现。该基准支持超过100种实验操作类型，代理的报告通过与隐藏的真实实验室记录进行对比来评分。每个任务都有一个独立的TwinLab代理，作为环境运行测试代理所设计的实验，读取实验室原始材料并调用由其记录构建的计算器。与仅看到任务描述的语言模型相比，TwinLab代理几乎消除了与真实记录不符的读数和重复请求不一致的答案，且通过闭卷探测控制了来自记忆的回忆。我们发现这些任务对前沿模型仍然具有挑战性：最强的Claude Fable 5.2平均得分仅为45.0，我们还对其失败原因进行了实证研究。
👤 Yang Wan, Linchao Zhu 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26084]
agentic-rl
RL Can Only Reinforce What It Sees Succeed
强化学习只能强化它所观察到的成功
When a familiar rule stops working, an agent has to weigh what pre-training taught it against what it now observes. We ask how outcome-reward reinforcement learning (RL) changes this balance, in a puzzle world where both sides are known: small language models trained from scratch on games played by a Bayesian player, so that the prior and the right answer are exact. Base models hedge as their corpus did, and RL turns that hedging into decisions, in whichever direction the reward favours. But RL can only reinforce what it sees succeed. When the right action is rare, each run either learns it everywhere or not at all, and which runs take off is predicted before training by one count: the expected number of groups of attempts in which the action wins. About 35 such groups give even odds. The count holds for new and repeated problems and for groups of 4 to 256 attempts, and across learning rates, optimizers, GRPO variants and model sizes from 1.3M to 85M parameters, which move the threshold but not what is counted.
中文翻译
当一个熟悉的规则失效时，代理必须权衡预训练所教内容与当前观察结果。我们探讨了结果奖励强化学习（RL）如何改变这一平衡，在一个已知双方信息的谜题世界中：使用从贝叶斯玩家游戏数据中从头训练的小型语言模型，使得先验知识和正确答案完全明确。基础模型沿袭其语料库的倾向，而RL则将其犹豫转化为决策，方向取决于奖励偏好。但RL只能强化它所观察到的成功。当正确动作稀少时，每次运行要么全学到，要么全学不到，而哪些运行能成功起飞，可在训练前通过一个计数预测：该动作获胜的尝试组的预期数量。约35组时概率为50%。该计数适用于新问题和重复问题，以及4至256次尝试的组别，且在学习率、优化器、GRPO变体及模型规模（1.3M至85M参数）变化下依然成立，尽管阈值会随这些因素移动，但计数本身不变。
👤 Ruben Sanchez-Corcuera, Unai Zulaika, Aritz Bilbao-Jayo, Miguel Fernandez-de-Retana 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.25989]
agentic-rl
Policy Improvement-Guided Replay for Continual Reinforcement Learning
持续强化学习中的策略改进引导回放
Continual reinforcement learning (CRL) requires models to acquire new capabilities while retaining performance on previous tasks. A stability-oriented perspective common in conventional continual learning emphasizes knowledge preservation and interference mitigation. Complementing this perspective, we revisit CRL for vision-language models through policy improvement, a fundamental principle of RL, to preserve acquired performance gains. Using the performance difference lemma, we characterize historical performance changes through the current policy's expected advantages on historical task distributions, motivating replay of historical prompts with the current policy. To allocate limited replay memory, we propose Contrastive Likelihood Replay (CoLiR), which contrasts likelihood gains on correct responses from the final task policy with likelihood reductions on incorrect responses from the initial policy. Our analysis links correct-response likelihood gains to reference-policy advantages under an idealized KL-regularized optimal update and provides an endogenous-reward interpretation for general policy updates. CoLiR estimates scores from finite training rollouts and cached log-likelihoods, selects prompts at task boundaries, and replays them uniformly during subsequent training, with KL regularization restricted to replayed prompts. Memory construction only requires a small amount of additional cross-policy scoring but no additional response generation. Across three Qwen3-VL model sizes on MRCL, CoLiR achieves the highest mean final accuracy and mean task accuracy among the compared methods while maintaining competitive new-task performance.
中文翻译
持续强化学习（CRL）要求模型在获得新能力的同时保留对先前任务的性能。传统持续学习中常见的以稳定性为导向的观点强调知识保存和干扰缓解。我们通过强化学习的基本原则——策略改进，重新审视视觉语言模型的CRL，以保持已获得的性能提升。利用性能差异引理，我们通过当前策略在历史任务分布上的期望优势来刻画历史性能变化，从而提出使用当前策略回放历史提示。为有限的回放内存分配，我们提出了对比似然回放（CoLiR），该方法对比最终任务策略在正确回答上的似然增益与初始策略在错误回答上的似然降低。我们的分析将正确回答的似然增益与理想化的KL正则化最优更新下的参考策略优势联系起来，并为一般策略更新提供了内生奖励解释。CoLiR从有限训练轨迹和缓存的对数似然中估计得分，在任务边界选择提示，并在后续训练中均匀回放，同时将KL正则化限制在回放提示上。内存构建仅需少量额外的跨策略评分，无需额外的回答生成。在MRCL数据集上对三个Qwen3-VL模型尺寸的实验表明，CoLiR在所有对比方法中实现了最高的平均最终准确率和平均任务准确率，同时保持了有竞争力的新任务性能。
👤 Qin-Wen Luo, Haoxuan Gao, Sheng Ren, Xiang Chen, Sheng-Jun Huang 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

RouteVerify: Routing Interventions for Step-Level Verification in Mixture-of-Experts Models
RouteVerify：在专家混合模型中进行步骤级验证的路由干预
Routing states in mixture-of-experts (MoE) models can distinguish correct from erroneous reasoning steps. If changing routing also changes a model's step-level verification judgment, it is tempting to conclude that the judgment follows what routing encodes. We test this inference with RouteVerify, a controlled intervention study that replaces selected internal activations from one solution trace with activations from another, while keeping the evaluated input unchanged. Across nine MoE checkpoints, we compare three intervention interfaces: router logits, MoE outputs, and post-MoE residual states. We vary the layers and token positions at which activations are replaced. We measure changes in a verification margin that quantifies the model's preference for INCORRECT over CORRECT. We also evaluate the same input with activations from correct versus erroneous steps to test whether the resulting judgment depends on the source step's correctness. We report four linked results. First, probes on router logits separate correct from erroneous steps in eight checkpoints (AUROC 0.80-0.88). Second, replacing router logits across all routed layers changes the judgment in all nine checkpoints, and by more than a norm-matched random replacement. Third, this change rarely depends on whether the source step is correct. At one layer, routing from erroneous steps and from correct steps produces indistinguishable margins in all nine checkpoints. Under all-layer routing, their difference is resolved and positive in only one. Fourth, the same difference is positive for local residual replacements in eight of nine checkpoints, so the measure does detect such dependence where it exists. Correctness is therefore readable from routing, and routing replacements move the judgment, but the movement rarely follows correctness.
中文翻译
专家混合（MoE）模型中的路由状态可以区分正确的与错误的推理步骤。如果改变路由也改变了模型的步骤级验证判断，人们很容易得出结论：该判断遵循路由所编码的内容。我们通过RouteVerify这一受控干预研究对此推断进行了检验，该研究将某一解题轨迹中的选定内部激活替换为另一轨迹中的激活，同时保持待评估输入不变。在九个MoE检查点上，我们比较了三种干预接口：路由logits、MoE输出和后MoE残差状态。我们还改变了激活替换的层和标记位置。我们测量了验证置信度的变化，该指标量化了模型对错误答案相对于正确答案的偏好程度。此外，我们还用正确步骤和错误步骤的激活对同一输入进行评估，以测试最终判断是否依赖于源步骤的正确性。我们报告了四项相互关联的结果：第一，在八个检查点中，对路由logits的探测能够有效区分正确与错误步骤（AUROC为0.80–0.88）；第二，在所有路由层上替换路由logits会改变所有九个检查点的判断，且变化幅度超过与范数匹配的随机替换；第三，这种变化很少依赖于源步骤是否正确；在一个层上，来自错误步骤和正确步骤的路由在所有九个检查点中产生的置信度差异无法区分；在全层路由下，只有其中一个检查点显示出正向差异；第四，在九个检查点中有八个检查点中，局部残差替换的差异为正，说明该度量确实能检测到存在的依赖关系。因此，正确性可以从路由中读取，路由替换也会改变判断，但这种变化很少遵循正确性本身。
👤 Yucheng Du, Ryan A. Rossi, Xiyang Hu 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

LM-based Personalized Travel Planner
基于大语言模型的个性化旅行规划器
Effective travel planning requires handling multiple information sources, managing constraints, and tailoring recommendations to user preferences. While LLMs have introduced dynamic, context-aware decision-making, single-agent models often struggle with complex multi-step reasoning, constraint adherence, and modular execution. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hierarchical multi-agent framework where a supervising chatbot agent coordinates specialized agents for schedule validation, user query formulation, real-time data retrieval, and constraint-aware itinerary generation. By integrating LLM reasoning, external APIs, and iterative refinement mechanisms based on a deterministic, multi-dimensional scoring framework, our approach ensures adaptability, efficiency, and personalization. Evaluations on the well-known TravelPlanner benchmark demonstrate improvements in Budget Constraint planning by 10\%, wider constraints set-based planning by 25\%, and personalized planning results by 45\%. compared to the ReAct baseline. Our code is available at https://github.com/nimashaklaa/TravelPlanner_Research

Pro$^2$ Agent: Scientific Problem Probing for Autonomous AI R&D
Pro$^2$ 智能体：面向自主AI研发的科学问题探查
Large language model (LLM) agents can now autonomously conduct AI research by writing code and running experiments. They typically do so by iteratively optimizing a codebase, while ignoring the problem structure behind the task. This formulation is effective on simpler tasks, but on difficult problems it often gets stuck in the wrong solution region because the agent never explicitly represents its understanding of the problem. We introduce Pro$^2$ Agent, which shifts the primary state of autonomous AI research from a codebase state to a Problem State: an explicit structure of what problems prevent success and which solutions are likely to address them. Pro$^2$ Agent initializes the Problem State through comprehensive analysis of available resources and constraints. It then performs targeted research to relate the identified problems to candidate solutions. Through controlled hypothesis testing, the agent updates the importance of a suspected problem or the benefit brought by a candidate solution. On the complete MLE-Bench, Pro$^2$ Agent matches the best reported medal rate (70.7%) under the standard 24-hour budget and reaches 76.0% with a 48-hour budget, the highest medal rate reported on the complete benchmark. It also achieves competitive performance on PostTrainBench. In addition, it produces research notes for each task that make the research process transparent and reusable by human researchers.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLM）智能体现在能够通过编写代码和运行实验自主开展AI研究。它们通常通过迭代优化代码库来完成任务，却忽视了任务背后的结构。这种范式在简单任务上有效，但在困难问题上常因智能体未显式表示对问题的理解而陷入错误解空间。我们提出Pro$^2$智能体，将自主AI研究的主要状态从代码库状态转变为“问题状态”：即明确表达阻碍成功的具体问题及可能解决这些问题的方案结构。Pro$^2$智能体通过全面分析可用资源与约束初始化问题状态，随后有针对性地研究将识别出的问题与候选解决方案关联起来。通过受控假设检验，智能体更新可疑问题的重要性或候选方案带来的收益。在完整的MLE-Bench上，Pro$^2$智能体在标准24小时预算下达到最高报告奖牌率（70.7%），在48小时预算下达到76.0%，为完整基准上报告的最高奖牌率。它还在PostTrainBench上表现出色。此外，它为每个任务生成的研究笔记使研究过程透明且可供人类研究人员复用。
👤 Yixiong Chen, Liqian Peng, Tian Tan, Ziqian Lin, Blake Jianhang Chen et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv

Steering Automated Scientific Discovery via Audited Epistemic Exploration
通过审计的认知探索引导自动化科学发现
Scientific discovery requires revisiting the hypotheses in light of new evidence and identifying promising directions beyond those already explored. However, research agents often make claims that are not supported by their observations, explore a narrow hypothesis space, and retain beliefs despite recognizing conflicting evidence. These failures motivate the search for an explicit epistemic state that captures both beliefs about existing hypotheses and directions for further exploration. To this end, we propose Epistemic Compass (EpiCompass), a plug-and-play scaffold that maintains this state through an epistemic tree linking hypotheses, evidence, beliefs, and revisions. As an investigation progresses, EpiCompass reassesses the hypotheses explored against accumulated evidence and uses unresolved conflicts and unexplored directions to guide hypothesis generation. It selects candidates by anticipated epistemic gain, accounting for both refinement of existing hypotheses and expansion of the hypothesis space, and verifies the final conclusion against the accumulated evidence. Experimental results across three scientific discovery benchmarks and three LLM backbones demonstrate that EpiCompass consistently outperforms baselines, with further analyses showing reductions in errors of unsupported claims and failures to revise beliefs.
中文翻译
科学发现需要根据新证据重新审视假设，并识别超出已有探索范围的有前景方向。然而，研究智能体常做出无证据支持的断言，探索狭窄的假设空间，并在认识到矛盾证据后仍固守原有信念。这些失败促使我们寻找一种显式的认知状态，以同时捕捉对现有假设的信念以及进一步探索的方向。为此，我们提出Epistemic Compass（EpiCompass），一种即插即用的框架，通过认知树维护这一状态，该树连接假设、证据、信念与修订。随着调查推进，EpiCompass根据累积证据重新评估已探索的假设，并利用未解决的冲突和未探索的方向引导新假设生成。它通过预期认知收益选择候选假设，兼顾现有假设的精炼与假设空间的拓展，并最终用累积证据验证结论。在三个科学发现基准和三种LLM骨干网络上的实验结果表明，EpiCompass始终优于基线，进一步分析显示其显著减少了无依据断言错误和信念不修正的失败。
👤 Fangxu Yu, Tao Feng, Xuying Ning, Haolin Yang, Jiaxuan You et al. 📅 2026-11-18 🔗 arXiv

Learning to Read Events for Exact State Tracking with Frozen Language Models
使用冻结语言模型进行精确状态追踪的事件读取学习
Long event streams require identifying each change and applying the changes in order. We train a selector on a frozen language model to read each transition. With API-only access, we ask the model to list the transitions. An external integer register composes them in order. A confidence rule can flag uncertain updates or retain several candidates. We study products of permutations of five items, a task that fixed-depth, log-precision transformers cannot solve on all input lengths under the standard conjecture that $\mathrm{TC}^0 \ne \mathrm{NC}^1$. On two-event windows, a 27B model answered 3 of 100 correctly without visible reasoning and 96 of 100 when it generated intermediate steps. A frozen 0.8B model with a trained selector read every event in 299 of 299 windows of 43,687 events each. The same package passed 298 of 299 new windows on a fresh machine, missing the target of 0.99 for the one-sided 95% lower confidence bound. A separate follow-up sized to allow one failure yielded 472 of 473. On 1,000-event room-swap documents, three of ten models solved all five documents by reasoning alone. All seven models tested both ways at this length solved all five with extraction and the register, with no chain of thought requested. With concurrent extraction, median wall-clock time fell from 136.1 to 10.4 s for Sonnet 5.5 and from 313.5 to 28.0 s for GPT-6.1 Sol. At fixed per-token rates, mean cost per document fell by 55% for Sonnet 5.5 and 44% for GPT-6.1 Sol. A regular expression with an executor also solved all 15 fixed-format documents. On 500 free-form issue-tracker comments, a separately adapted 4B reader identified the recorded value in 68.8% of cases, compared with 61.0% for a frequency baseline fitted on the same training projects.
中文翻译
长事件流需要识别每个变化并按顺序应用。我们在一个冻结的语言模型上训练一个选择器来读取每个转换。仅通过API访问，要求模型列出所有转换。外部整数寄存器按顺序组合这些转换。置信度规则可标记不确定的更新或保留多个候选。我们研究了五个项目排列的乘积，该任务在标准假设TC⁰ ≠ NC¹下，固定深度、对数精度的Transformer无法在所有输入长度上解决。在双事件窗口中，一个27B模型在未显示推理的情况下正确回答了100个中的3个，而在生成中间步骤时正确回答了96个。一个经过训练选择器的0.8B冻结模型在43,687个事件的299个窗口中读取了每一个事件。同一套系统在新机器上对299个新窗口的测试中通过了298个，仅低于0.99的一侧95%置信下限目标一个；另一项专门设计允许一次失败的实验则通过了472/473。在1,000事件的房间交换文档中，十种模型中有三种仅通过推理就解决了全部五个文档。所有七种模型在该长度下均以提取和寄存器方式成功解决全部五个文档，且无需链式思维请求。并发提取使Sonnet 5.5的平均墙钟时间从136.1秒降至10.4秒，GPT-6.1 Sol从313.5秒降至28.0秒。在每token成本固定的情况下，Sonnet 5.5的平均文档成本下降55%，GPT-6.1 Sol下降44%。一个带有执行器的正则表达式也成功解决了全部15个固定格式文档。在500条自由格式的问题跟踪评论中，一个独立适配的4B阅读器在68.8%的情况下识别出记录值，优于在同一训练项目上拟合的频率基线（61.0%）。
👤 Jeonghoon Lee 📅 2026-11-18 🔗 arXiv
[2611.25167]

Game-Theoretic Data Allocation for Expert Specialization and Routing
基于博弈论的数据分配以实现专家专业化与路由
Can the allocation used to train experts also predict which expert will perform best on a prompt? Such a connection would make the intended division of labor inspectable before training and allow the allocation itself to guide inference. We introduce InflEns, a mixture of LoRA experts whose training weights and inference routes come from the same location game in a fixed prompt-trait space. Players choose positions to maximize their expected shares of the prompt distribution, without using expert losses. We utilize the game's kernel-general theoretical criterion for when co-located positions become locally unstable to separation. We evaluate InflEns on a corpus assembled from seven safety-related benchmarks, which demonstrates that the highest-share expert has the lowest held-out response negative log-likelihood (NLL) on approximately 90% of prompts across four kernels under a sharpened simplex representation, compared with approximately 15% after permuting allocations across prompts. We demonstrate that the method performs comparably to published learned gates without having to train an explicit gate on the experts. Because the router is fixed, we can trace harmful behavior to a single expert and retrain only that expert, which lowers HarmBench attack success from 0.51 to 0.02 while keeping instruction following. The game also gives us several controls over how the experts specialize, including the kernel choice and its parameters, the trait-space geometry, and the number of experts. Importantly, InflEns is a general framework that can be extended to any encoder and data distribution.
中文翻译
用于训练专家的数据分配是否也能预测哪个专家在特定提示下表现最佳？若存在这种关联，可在训练前揭示预期分工，并使分配本身指导推理过程。我们提出InflEns，一种LoRA专家混合模型，其训练权重和推理路由均来自固定提示特征空间中的位置博弈。参与者选择位置以最大化其在提示分布中的期望份额，而不依赖专家损失。我们利用博弈的核泛化理论准则，判断共处位置何时在局部变得不稳定而分离。我们在由七个安全相关基准组成的语料库上评估InflEns，结果表明，在四种核函数下，采用锐化单纯形表示法时，高份额专家在约90%的提示中具有最低的保留响应负对数似然（NLL），而将分配随机打乱后仅约15%。我们证明该方法性能可媲美已发表的显式门控机制，且无需对专家训练显式门控。由于路由器是固定的，我们可以将有害行为追溯至单一专家并仅重训该专家，从而将HarmBench攻击成功率从0.51降至0.02，同时保持指令遵循能力。该博弈还提供了多种控制专家专业化的手段，包括核选择及其参数、特征空间几何结构以及专家数量。重要的是，InflEns是一个通用框架，可扩展至任意编码器和数据分布。
👤 Mark Lovett, Denis Lim, Soroush Vosoughi 📅 2026-11-18 🔗 arXiv
[2611.25135]

Calibrated Decision Rewards: Training Language Models with a Judge That Knows How Sure It Is
校准决策奖励：使用知道自身确定性的裁判训练语言模型
Reinforcement learning (RL) can only teach a language model what its reward can recognize. Answer checkers are exact but need a reference answer; learned reward models and LLM judges work everywhere, but their scores are not probabilities. We study RLCDR (Reinforcement Learning from Calibrated Decision Rewards). An LLM converted into a decision model answers a typed question about a response ("is the final answer correct?", "which step is the first error?") with a probability distribution, read in one forward pass, debiased over option orders and calibrated with about a hundred labels. Training Qwen3-1.7B with GRPO on MATH, this reference-free reward raises MATH500 accuracy from 72.2% to 76.8% (+4.65 points, p < 1e-4), matching training on the answer key (76.9%), while a random reward gains only +0.65. The gain grows with difficulty, from 44.8% to 51.7% on the hardest level. On the policy's own changing samples the reward stays calibrated: its expected calibration error is half that of a softmax judge on the same 27B backbone (0.074 vs. 0.141), and it shows no sign of reward hacking. Without step training, the same model finds the first wrong step better than a dedicated 7B process reward model (ProcessBench F1 0.772 vs. 0.722, 20/20 splits). We also map where the approach stops being enough: trained only on problems the base model mostly fails, the reference-free reward still improves the policy (+1.9 MATH500 points, p = 0.03) but trails the answer key, because its probabilities become overconfident on that distribution. We state six hypotheses in advance and test each with a control designed to falsify it.
中文翻译
强化学习（RL）只能教会语言模型其奖励函数所能识别的内容。答案检查器准确但需参考答案；学习的奖励模型和LLM裁判虽可普遍适用，但其得分并非概率。我们研究RLCDR（基于校准决策奖励的强化学习）。将LLM转化为决策模型，通过单次前向传播回答关于响应的类型化问题（“最终答案是否正确？”、“哪一步是第一个错误？”），并输出概率分布，经选项顺序去偏并用约一百个标签进行校准。在MATH数据集上使用GRPO训练Qwen3-1.7B模型，此无参考奖励将MATH500准确率从72.2%提升至76.8%（+4.65分，p < 1e-4），接近使用答案键训练的结果（76.9%），而随机奖励仅提升+0.65。收益随难度增加：在最困难级别，准确率从44.8%升至51.7%。在策略自身不断变化的样本上，该奖励仍保持校准：其期望校准误差仅为相同27B主干模型上softmax裁判的一半（0.074 vs. 0.141），且未表现出奖励劫持迹象。在不进行步骤训练的情况下，同一模型在识别首个错误步骤方面优于专用的7B流程奖励模型（ProcessBench F1 0.772 vs. 0.722，20/20划分）。我们还确定了该方法失效的边界：仅在基础模型大多失败的问题上训练时，无参考奖励仍能提升策略（+1.9 MATH500分，p = 0.03），但落后于答案键，因为其在该分布上的概率变得过度自信。我们提前提出六个假设，并为每个假设设计对照实验以验证其真伪。
👤 Rudransh Agnihotri, Manasvi Kapoor 📅 2026-11-18 🔗 arXiv

[2611.26492]
Multimodal Error Detection in Chest X-ray Reports: A Large Language Model Reading Image Evidence
多模态胸部X光报告中的错误检测：大型语言模型读取图像证据
Rad-Linter checks chest X-ray reports against image-derived evidence to flag potential errors for review. A pretrained image classifier supplies scores for nine pathologies, and a 1.5B-parameter language model, fine-tuned with LoRA on 1,816 examples, checks their consistency with the report. We compare it with a keyword-and-negation rule baseline given the same reports and scores. Evaluation uses controlled report-score pairings from OpenI, with consistency labels derived from automatic report labels and score thresholds. Each counterfactual quad crosses two real reports with two real score vectors from other patients, creating two consistent and two inconsistent pairs. Every report and score vector occurs in both conditions, so either input alone permits only 50% accuracy. On 131 quads, Rad-Linter achieves 98.5% pair accuracy versus 80.5% for the rule baseline, and classifies all four pairs correctly in 94.4% of quads versus 56.5%. The checkers perform similarly on omission items, including unmentioned and negated findings; larger differences occur on the remaining item types, which require interpreting how findings are described. The same language model without fine-tuning remains near 50% accuracy. On a second set of 181 quads that also includes reports on which the automatic labelers disagree, pair accuracy is 93.4% versus 75.3%. These controlled results show that, given the same image-derived scores, a trained language model detects report-score inconsistencies more accurately than the tested rule baseline, with the advantage concentrated in interpreting how findings are worded.
中文翻译
Rad-Linter通过对比胸部X光报告与图像推导出的证据来标记潜在错误以供审查。一个预训练的图像分类器提供九种病理的评分，一个经过LoRA微调的15亿参数语言模型检查这些评分与报告的一致性。我们将该方法与基于关键词和否定规则的基线进行比较，使用相同的报告和评分。评估采用来自OpenI的受控报告-评分配对，一致性标签由自动报告标签和评分阈值推导得出。每个反事实四元组包含两个真实报告与另外两名患者的两个真实评分向量，形成两对一致和两对不一致的组合。每份报告和评分向量在两种条件下均出现，因此单独依赖任一输入最多只能达到50%准确率。在131个四元组上，Rad-Linter的配对准确率达到98.5%，而规则基线为80.5%；在94.4%的四元组中，Rad-Linter能正确分类全部四对，而规则基线仅为56.5%。在遗漏项（包括未提及和否定发现）上，两者表现相似；而在其余需要解释发现描述方式的项目类型上差异更大。未经微调的同一语言模型准确率仍接近50%。在第二组181个四元组中，还包括自动标注者存在分歧的报告，Rad-Linter的配对准确率为93.4%，规则基线为75.3%。这些受控结果表明，在给定相同的图像推导评分的前提下，经过训练的语言模型比测试的规则基线更准确地检测报告与评分之间的不一致，优势集中在对发现表述方式的理解上。
👤 Yanda Cheng, Bo Wen, Yuhao Lu 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26317]
Language-Oriented Semantic Modeling for Target Language Extraction
面向目标语言提取的语言导向语义建模
Target Language Extraction (TLE) aims to isolate speech in a target language from multilingual mixtures. Existing approaches primarily rely on acoustic modeling with language identity labels, which provide only coarse categorical guidance. In this work, we propose a language-oriented semantic modeling framework that augments acoustic extraction with language-specific semantic representations. A pre-trained speech semantic model is adapted via language-aware prompt conditioning and parameter-efficient fine-tuning to generate target-language-focused semantic representations from the mixture. {These capture high-level semantic structure of the acoustics and they} are integrated into the extraction model to provide structured semantic guidance beyond identity labels. Experiments on the CommonVoiceMix dataset show that incorporating semantic representations consistently improves over acoustic-only baselines, achieving up to 1.16 dB SI-SDR gain.
中文翻译
目标语言提取（TLE）旨在从多语言语音混合中分离出目标语言的语音。现有方法主要依赖带有语言身份标签的声学建模，仅提供粗粒度的类别指导。本文提出一种语言导向的语义建模框架，通过引入语言特异的语义表示来增强声学提取。利用预训练的语音语义模型，通过语言感知的提示调制和参数高效微调，从混合信号中生成聚焦目标语言的语义表示。这些表示捕捉了声学信号的高层语义结构，并被集成到提取模型中，提供超越身份标签的结构化语义指导。在 CommonVoiceMix 数据集上的实验表明，引入语义表示能持续优于仅依赖声学的基线，最高实现1.16 dB的SI-SDR提升。
👤 Mehmet Sinan Yıldırım, Ke Zhang, Shuai Wang, Haizhou Li 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26286]
Twice-Told Critiques: Claim-Level Homogenization of Peer Review in the LLM Era
两次重复的评审：大语言模型时代同行评审中的主张级同质化
Peer review relies on several readers finding different problems. We measure critique overlap in conference and journal reviews. At ICLR, measured overlap rose from 12.0% in 2026 to 21.3% in 2027, in reviews from the same stage of the review process, with year-on-year rises at NeurIPS, ICML and the F1000 and PLOS journals. A third ICLR review of a paper added 5.46 new problems to the other two in 2027, against 6.12 in 2026. We apply MELD, a detector of LLM-generated text, to all reviews. Within a paper, two reviews that MELD flags overlap more than two unflagged reviews: by 3.0 points at ICLR in 2024-2025, by 4.5 points in 2026-2027, and by 5.4 points across five journal sources. These results provide evidence that LLM-flagged reviews of one paper are less distinct from each other than unflagged reviews, and suggest that the diversity of critiques among reviews of a single paper is falling.
中文翻译
同行评审依赖于多位审稿人发现不同问题。我们测量了会议和期刊评审中的评审意见重叠程度。在 ICLR 中，同一评审阶段的评审重叠率从2026年的12.0%上升至2027年的21.3%，NeurIPS、ICML 以及 F1000 和 PLOS 期刊也呈现逐年上升趋势。2027年，一篇论文的第三轮评审相比前两轮新增了5.46个问题，而2026年为6.12个。我们将 MELD（一种检测大语言模型生成文本的工具）应用于所有评审。对于同一论文，MELD 标记的两份评审之间的重叠度高于未标记的两份评审：在 ICLR 2024–2025 年间高出3.0点，在2026–2027年间高出4.5点，在五个期刊来源中整体高出5.4点。这些结果表明，被 MELD 标记的同一论文的评审之间差异更小，提示单一论文的评审意见多样性正在下降。
👤 Ilya Gusev 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26255]
STAGE: Spatio-Temporal Augmented Graph Expansion for Weakly Supervised Cross-Tweet Event Causality Discovery
STAGE：用于弱监督跨推文事件因果发现的时空增强图扩展
Causal relations in disaster-related social media of- ten span independent posts, making cross-tweet causal discovery difficult to supervise and model at scale. We propose STAGE, a framework that separates scalable cross-tweet weak-supervision construction from spatio-temporal relational learning over event occurrences. Starting from local causal seeds, STAGE retrieves semantically related event occurrences across tweets and uses a causal scorer to construct weakly labeled cross-tweet pairs. These pairs supervise a multi-head Graph Attention Network over semantically enriched event representations and a spatio- temporal context graph. We evaluate STAGE on California Wildfire and Hurricane Harvey using human-gold cross-tweet test sets and 15 baselines. Compared with local-only supervision, the constructed cross-tweet supervision improves STAGE AUPRC by 0.340 on Wildfire and 0.107 on Hurricane. STAGE achieves in-domain AUROC of 0.885 on Wildfire and 0.710 on Hurricane. Cross-disaster experiments reveal asymmetric transfer, highlighting sensitivity to dataset-specific spatio-temporal shifts. These results demonstrate the value of scalable cross-tweet supervision combined with relational event modeling for causal learning across fragmented disaster reports.
中文翻译
灾难相关社交媒体中的因果关系往往跨越独立的推文，使得大规模的跨推文因果发现难以进行监督和建模。我们提出了STAGE框架，将可扩展的跨推文弱监督构建与事件发生情况的时空关系学习分离。从局部因果种子出发，STAGE在推文中检索语义相关的事件实例，并使用因果评分器构建弱标注的跨推文配对。这些配对用于监督基于语义丰富事件表示的多头图注意力网络以及时空上下文图。我们在加州山火和哈维飓风数据集上使用人工黄金标准的跨推文测试集和15个基线对STAGE进行了评估。相比仅局部监督，所构建的跨推文监督使STAGE在山火数据上的AUPRC提升0.340，在飓风数据上提升0.107。STAGE在山火数据上达到0.885的域内AUROC，在飓风数据上为0.710。跨灾难实验揭示了不对称迁移现象，凸显了对数据集特定时空变化的敏感性。这些结果表明，结合可扩展的跨推文监督与关系事件建模对于碎片化灾难报告中的因果学习具有重要价值。
👤 Hieu Minh Duong, Todd Gary, Eugene Levin, Long Nguyen 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26216]
Frontier models need only one text to unmask its human or AI author
前沿模型仅需一条文本即可识别其人类或AI作者
Given a single text and a simple zero-shot prompt (no web access, tools, candidate lists, or writing samples), frontier models can answer "who wrote this?" at three increasingly specific levels: (1) whether an AI or human wrote it, (2) if an AI, which lab, and (3) if a human, often exactly who. The latest models (Claude Fable 5.2, GPT-6.1 Sol, Gemini 4 Carbon) match trained detectors like Pangram while also explaining their predictions in natural language, and Opus 5.5 names the correct lab for 98% of texts generated by models from Anthropic, OpenAI, and Google. Most strikingly, Fable 5.2 correctly names the author of 39% of public peer reviews published in Oct-Nov 2026 (78% on a subset of reviews from Nature), and identifies the username behind 69% of comments from active Redditors (with >500 posts over the last year). Accuracy depends on exposure to an author's writing in pretraining data and on reasoning effort, and simply asking a model how familiar it is with someone predicts whether it can identify their writing. For anyone who has published enough writing online, frontier models present a greater threat to anonymity than commonly assumed, and defenses are limited.
中文翻译
给定一条文本和一个简单的零样本提示（无需网络访问、工具、候选列表或写作样本），前沿模型能够以三个越来越具体的层次回答“谁写的？”：（1）是AI还是人类所写；（2）如果是AI，来自哪个实验室；（3）如果是人类，通常能准确指出具体是谁。最新模型（Claude Fable 5.2、GPT-6.1 Sol、Gemini 4 Carbon）在性能上媲美训练好的检测器如Pangram，同时还能用自然语言解释其预测。Opus 5.5在Anthropic、OpenAI和Google生成的98%文本中正确识别出所属实验室。最引人注目的是，Fable 5.2成功识别出2026年10月至11月期间发表的公开同行评审中39%的作者（Nature期刊子集达78%），并识别出69%活跃Reddit用户的用户名（过去一年发帖超过500条）。准确性取决于模型在预训练数据中对作者写作风格的暴露程度以及推理努力程度，仅询问模型对某人写作风格的熟悉度即可预测其是否能识别该作者。对于在线发表过足够多内容的人而言，前沿模型对匿名性的威胁远超普遍认知，且现有防御手段极为有限。
👤 Atrey Desai, Deniz Böloni-Turgut, Ty Brassington, Minjoon Choi, Jenna Russell et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26077]
Same Judgement, Different Scores: Numerical Scoring Topology in LLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation
相同判断，不同分数：LLM作为评判者评估中的数值评分拓扑
LLMs are increasingly used as automated evaluators to reduce the cost and time required for human annotation. However, the role of scoring-scheme design in the reliability of LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation remains underexplored. To address this problem, we introduce numerical scoring topology, which characterizes the structural design of a scoring scheme in terms of its scale width, granularity, offset, symmetry, and boundary placement. Building on this definition, we conduct the first systematic study of how numerical scoring topology influences zero-shot LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation across diverse benchmarks and judge models. Our results show that numerical scoring topology systematically affects judge scores, calibration, and robustness, particularly in judgment-intensive evaluations. Shifted and narrow ranges tend to induce some of the largest score deviations across many settings. We further find that stronger and larger judge models generally achieve higher discriminative validity and greater robustness to scoring-topology changes. These findings suggest that numerical scoring topology should be treated as an integral component of LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation and highlight the need for more principled and standardized scoring protocols.
中文翻译

[2611.26075]
Understanding MoE Decisions Beyond Gradients
超越梯度理解MoE决策
Understanding a language model's decision requires knowing how its internal computations affect its preference between answers. Gradient-based methods estimate this influence by predicting the effect of changing those computations. In mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, replacing one expert can change which experts later layers select. Those switches can alter the answer scores in ways that derivatives do not capture. We introduce a predictor that uses the model's own expert outputs and selection rules to estimate these extra effects, then adds them to a second-order prediction. Across questions from three benchmarks, our method reduces the error in predicting answer-score changes by 60\% on Qwen3.6 and 47\% on Gemma4, relative to a multistep baseline using gradients and second derivatives.
中文翻译
理解语言模型的决策需要了解其内部计算如何影响对答案的偏好。基于梯度的方法通过预测改变这些计算的影响来估计这种影响。在混合专家（MoE）模型中，替换一个专家可能改变后续层选择的专家。这些切换会以导数无法捕捉的方式改变答案得分。我们提出一种预测器，利用模型自身的专家输出和选择规则来估计这些额外影响，然后将其加入二阶预测中。在三个基准测试的问题上，我们的方法相对于使用梯度和二阶导数的多步基线，在Qwen3.6上将答案得分变化预测误差降低了60%，在Gemma4上降低了47%。
👤 Zhen Xu, Zitao Zhang, Kevin Zhao, Xuefeng Liu 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26073]
AltRA-Test: An Alternative Annotator Test for LLM Judges Without Shared Annotators
AltRA-Test：无需共享标注者的LLM评判者替代标注者检验
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges and graders to replace human annotators, raising the question of when such replacement is statistically justified. To support such decisions, the Alternative Annotator Test (Alt-Test) was introduced for dense settings where all items are annotated by the same annotators. However, in many large-scale annotation settings, items are annotated by different subsets of annotators. We therefore propose the Alternative Annotator Test with Resampled Annotations (AltRA-Test), a statistical test that does not require that all items are annotated by the same annotators. AltRA-Test uses a bootstrap that resamples items, draws k human annotations for each item, and substitutes one of them with an LLM annotation. This produces paired all-human and mixed human-LLM panels. A replacement is supported when the inter-annotator agreement (IAA) of the mixed panel is statistically non-inferior to the IAA of the all-human panel, i.e., when the LLM preserves the signal that the human annotators share. We establish that AltRA-Test is a consistent test of asymptotic level alpha. We then evaluate it through simulations and real-world experiments in which we test 18 LLMs and 5 prompting techniques on 20 datasets across text, image, audio, and video. Across these evaluations, about 100 items often suffice for the test. In simulations, where we know whether the LLM is a better or a worse annotator than the humans, AltRA-Test makes more correct replacement decisions than existing baselines. In real-world data experiments, LLMs pass the test in about half of the setups, depending mainly on the task. Since replacement is not always justified, we encourage researchers to test it for their task.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地被用作评判者和评分者以替代人工标注者，这引发了何时此类替代在统计上是合理的疑问。为支持此类决策，已提出替代标注者检验（Alt-Test），适用于所有项目由相同标注者标注的密集场景。然而，在许多大规模标注场景中，项目由不同标注者子集标注。因此，我们提出替代标注者检验与重采样标注（AltRA-Test），一种不要求所有项目由相同标注者标注的统计检验。AltRA-Test采用Bootstrap重采样：对每个项目重采样，抽取k个真人标注，并用一个LLM标注替代其中一个，从而生成成对的全真人和混合真人-LLM标注小组。当混合小组的标注者间一致性（IAA）在统计上不劣于全真人小组时，即认为LLM保留了人类标注者共享的信号，此时可支持替代。我们证明AltRA-Test是一个渐近水平α的一致性检验。随后通过模拟和真实世界实验评估，测试了18个LLM和5种提示技术在20个涵盖文本、图像、音频和视频的数据集上的表现。在这些评估中，约100个样本通常已足够。在模拟中，已知LLM优于或劣于人类的情况下，AltRA-Test比现有基线做出更多正确替代决策。在真实数据实验中，LLM在约一半设置中通过测试，主要取决于任务类型。由于替代并非总是合理，我们鼓励研究者针对自身任务进行测试。
👤 Nagham Omar, Rotem Dror, Almog Gueta, Avigdor Gal, Roi Reichart et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.26009]
Evaluating safeguarding in multi-turn conversations with AI assistants
多轮对话中AI助手安全防护能力的评估
Conversational AI assistants are now used for personal and emotional support. Developers report improvements in how new models respond to sensitive conversations, yet users and civil society continue to raise concerns about their behaviour in real-world, repeated interactions that safety evaluations rarely capture. Often, evaluations present signs of distress clearly and in a single message, even though people may reveal their struggles gradually in otherwise ordinary conversations. We worked with three community mental health support workers to test if models provide the same level of safeguarding when cues are shared all at once or gradually over time. We designed nine scenarios in which a user shares cues related to depression, psychosis, or domestic abuse. For each scenario, the support workers established a gold standard response, ranging from signposting support to pausing the everyday task to address safeguarding. We presented five cues either together in one message or dispersed across a conversation. Across more than 7,400 conversations, we evaluated six widely used models from five developers. GPT-5.5, Grok 4.3, DeepSeek V4, and Llama 4 Maverick met the support workers' standards in just 26-59% of conversations when cues were concentrated, and only 3-6% when dispersed. Claude Sonnet 5 and Claude Opus 5 showed a smaller gap, meeting the standards in 68-97% of concentrated conversations and 59-87% of dispersed conversations. Even the best-performing model, Claude Opus 5, fell short of the gold standard in 13% of conversations when cues were dispersed. Safety evaluations centred on explicit, legible concerns can overstate how well safeguards protect people when signs of distress emerge gradually or indirectly through ordinary conversations. For claims of improved safety to be taken seriously, developers must design and assess safeguards around how people actually communicate.
中文翻译
对话式AI助手如今被用于个人及情感支持。开发者报告称新模型在应对敏感对话方面有所改进，但用户和民间社会仍持续关注其在真实世界、重复互动中的行为表现，而现有安全评估往往无法捕捉此类情境。通常评估仅在单条消息中明确呈现痛苦信号，然而现实中人们往往在日常对话中逐步透露困扰。我们与三位社区心理健康支持工作者合作，测试当线索一次性或分阶段呈现时，模型是否能提供同等水平的安全防护。设计了九种情景，涵盖抑郁、精神病或家庭暴力相关线索。每种情景下，支持工作者制定了黄金标准响应，包括引导求助、暂停日常任务以处理安全问题等。我们将五个线索集中于一条消息或分散在对话中呈现。在超过7,400次对话中评估了来自五家开发者的六款主流模型。GPT-5.5、Grok 4.3、DeepSeek V4和Llama 4 Maverick在线索集中的情况下仅在26%-59%的对话中达标，而在线索分散时仅3%-6%达标。Claude Sonnet 5和Claude Opus 5表现较好，集中线索下达标率为68%-97%，分散线索下为59%-87%。即使表现最佳的Claude Opus 5，在线索分散时仍有13%的对话未达黄金标准。基于显性、清晰关切的安全评估可能高估了防护措施在渐进或间接显现痛苦信号时的实际效果。若要严肃对待安全性能的改进声明，开发者必须围绕人们实际沟通方式来设计和评估安全机制。

Xing-Audio-NVVASR: Scaling and Refining Supervision for Joint Recognition of Speech and Non-Verbal Vocalizations
Xing-Audio-NVVASR：面向语音与非言语发声联合识别的规模化与精细化监督
Joint recognition of speech and non-verbal vocalizations (NVVs) is hindered by scarce event-rich training data and inaccurate or missing event labels. We present Xing-Audio-NVVASR, an end-to-end recognizer trained through progressive data mining and iterative supervision refinement. Guided by unified acoustic criteria, two heterogeneous models cross-audit annotations, flagging disagreements for teacher reannotation. To uncover shared omissions that model agreement may conceal, complementary breath detectors screen the entire corpus for human review. Human-confirmed labels accumulate across rounds and are preserved during subsequent refinement. Over five rounds, we refine annotations for 170,000 utterances from public corpora and incorporate 630,000 mined utterances. The resulting corpus of approximately 800,000 utterances is used to train a single 1.7B-parameter model that transcribes speech with inline tags for six NVV classes. Across four human-annotated benchmarks, Xing-Audio-NVVASR ranks first among nine models in both composite score and six-class Macro-F1. It achieves a mean composite score of 64.46, surpassing the strongest comparison model, StepAudio 3 Chat Preview, by 6.03 points, and exceeds the annotation teacher's breathing F1 by 14.40 to 31.57 percentage points. We plan to release the model weights, inference code, four evaluation benchmarks, annotation guidelines, and evaluation code.

[2611.25976]
Energy estimates of open-weight large language models across stated food weights: a dose-response evaluation
开放权重大语言模型在指定食物重量下的能量估算：剂量反应评估
We tested whether energy estimates of open-weight large language models (LLMs) scale proportionally with an exactly stated food weight, and whether computing energy density times weight removes the error. Under a prespecified protocol, seven open-weight LLMs estimated the energy of 168 randomly drawn foods from the USDA Food and Nutrient Database for Dietary Studies at nine stated weights, from 0.25 to 4 times the default amount. The primary outcome was the elasticity of the estimate with respect to weight (1: proportional scaling). A two-step estimate multiplied each model's own stated energy density by the weight in code. All seven models compressed their estimates, with elasticities from 0.548 to 0.979 (every 95% CI below 1); four compressed severely (0.548 to 0.709). These four overestimated a quarter of the default amount by 47% to 130% and underestimated four times it by 20% to 34%. Supplying the density restored near-proportional scaling in three of the four, and the two-step estimate cut the typical error factor at these extremes from 1.47-2.43 to 1.20-1.38. Compression persisted at full precision and grew in five of seven models under a plain question. The models hold the energy densities needed for proportional scaling and answer near a typical serving, reproducing the flat-slope syndrome of dietary recall. Accuracy at usual portions does not reveal it. Food-logging tools should compute the energy of weighed entries as density times weight, and evaluations should test fixed foods across stated weights.
中文翻译
我们检验了开放权重大语言模型（LLMs）的能量估算是否与精确声明的食物重量成比例，以及计算能量密度乘以重量能否消除误差。在预设协议下，七款开放权重LLM对美国农业部膳食研究食品与营养数据库中随机抽取的168种食物在九个声明重量（默认量的0.25至4倍）下的能量进行了估算。主要结果是估算值对重量的弹性（1: 比例缩放）。采用两步估算方法，即每个模型自身声明的能量密度乘以重量。所有七款模型均压缩了估算结果，弹性范围为0.548至0.979（95%置信区间均低于1），其中四款严重压缩（0.548至0.709）。这四款模型在默认量的四分之一时高估47%至130%，在四倍量时低估20%至34%。提供能量密度后，四款模型中有三款恢复了近似比例缩放；两步估算将极端情况下的典型误差因子从1.47-2.43降至1.20-1.38。在全精度下压缩现象依然存在，且在纯文本提问条件下，七款模型中有五款误差进一步扩大。这些模型具备实现比例缩放所需能量密度，且回答倾向于典型份量，重现了饮食回忆中的平斜综合征。常规份量下的准确性无法揭示此问题。食物记录工具应将称重条目的能量计算为密度乘以重量，评估也应测试固定食物在不同声明重量下的表现。

[2611.25970]
The Depth of a Prediction: Where Language Models Resolve a Word Predicts Human Reading Time
预测的深度：语言模型对词语预测的解析深度可预测人类阅读时间
Surprisal from a language model's final layer predicts how long people read a word, yet the model computes its prediction across many layers. We read each word's prediction at every layer with the tuned lens and summarize this layerwise surprisal trajectory by its resolution depth, the depth around which its change is centered. On eight English eye-tracking and self-paced reading corpora (81,957 words) and ten GPT-2, OPT and Pythia models, words that a model resolves later, and the words after them, are read more slowly than equally surprising words. Resolution depth improves held-out prediction beyond final-layer surprisal, entropy and the best single layer by 0.26 millinats per word (pooled p = 0.001), about a third of entropy's gain, and each model family gains, GPT-2 and Pythia through one corpus each. The net gain varies between corpora (random-effects mean 0.07, p = 0.37); against layer-shuffled depth, which adds as many coefficients, it is positive in seven of eight corpora (random-effects mean 0.23, p = 0.016), and untrained networks show no gain under the logit lens. Pooled over corpora, the effect holds with corrected word probabilities and beyond first-to-last-layer divergences, and a whole-trajectory functional regression adds no significant information beyond resolution depth.
中文翻译
语言模型最后一层的意外度（surprisal）可预测人们阅读一个词所需的时间，但模型是在多个层级上完成预测的。我们使用调优透镜在每一层读取每个词的预测，并通过其变化中心所在的层级——即“解析深度”——总结这一层间意外度轨迹。在八个英语眼动追踪和自定速阅读语料库（共81,957个词）以及十个GPT-2、OPT和Pythia模型上，模型解析更晚的词及其后续词，比同样意外度的词阅读得更慢。解析深度在保留预测上的表现优于最终层意外度、熵以及最佳单一层，每词提升0.26毫纳特（合并p=0.001），约为熵增益的三分之一；每种模型家族均获得收益（GPT-2和Pythia各通过一个语料库）。净增益在不同语料库间存在差异（随机效应均值0.07，p=0.37）；与打乱层级的深度相比，其在八组语料库中有七组为正（随机效应均值0.23，p=0.016），未经训练的网络在logit透镜下无增益。在语料库合并后，该效应在修正词概率和超越首尾层差异的情况下依然成立，且整体轨迹功能回归并未提供解析深度之外的显著信息。

[2611.25909]
The Linguistic Structure of Dependency-Conditioned Embeddings
依赖条件嵌入的语言结构
Dependency-based distributional models capture syntactically informed lexical similarities, but the geometric organisation of dependency-conditioned lexical representations remains underexplored. We investigate the global and local geometry of lemma-dependency relation embeddings using four representations trained on WikiText-103: two count-based PPMI configurations and two Skip-Gram with Negative Sampling (SGNS) configurations. At the global level, we analyse dependency-relation centroids, control for lexical identity through within-lemma centring, and examine their alignment with part-of-speech (POS) distributions. At the local level, we evaluate cross-relation geometric consistency, cross-method agreement, lexical similarity, and polysemous usages. Results show that global geometry is structured strongly by context POS across all representations. Locally, broader lexical similarity structures are partially preserved across dependency relations, while immediate neighbourhoods vary substantially, highlighting different usages. PPMI-based representations exhibit greater cross-relation consistency than SGNS-based representations, with cross-method agreement varying by relation. These findings reveal systematic linguistic organisation at multiple geometric scales and its sensitivity to representation choices.

基于依存关系的分布模型捕捉语法相关的词义相似性，但依赖条件下的词义表示的几何组织仍缺乏深入探索。我们利用在WikiText-103上训练的四种表示方法（两种基于计数的PPMI配置和两种负采样Skip-Gram（SGNS）配置），研究词元-依存关系嵌入的全局与局部几何结构。在全局层面，分析依存关系中心点，通过同词元中心化控制词汇身份，并考察其与词性（POS）分布的一致性。在局部层面，评估跨关系几何一致性、跨方法一致性、词义相似性以及多义用法。结果显示，所有表示中全局几何结构均强烈受上下文词性影响。局部上，更广泛的词义相似结构在不同依存关系间部分保留，而邻近区域差异显著，凸显不同用法。基于PPMI的表示在跨关系一致性上优于基于SGNS的表示，且跨方法一致性随关系类型而异。这些发现揭示了多层次几何尺度上的系统性语言组织及其对表示选择的敏感性。

[2611.25808]
Language models represent more about emphasis than they can tell you
语言模型所体现的强调信息远超其能表达的内容
Prosodic prominence can largely be predicted from the words of a sentence, but part of it depends on how the speaker reads it. We study how much of prominence language models recover from text, whether they can report it when asked, and what the speech signal adds. On read English speech labelled for word-level prominence, we compare frozen and fine-tuned text models, prompted large language models (LLMs) and speech models on the same words. All text models end within about one point of each other, at best 68.8% on the 3-way task, and largely make the same errors. With the prompts we tested, LLMs do no better than a word-majority baseline, even with 500 examples. Linear probes find more in their representations. Speech models reach 84.7%. We argue that text determines only part of prominence, and that the rest has to be heard. LLMs encode what the text determines, but prompting does not bring it out: they represent more about where the emphasis falls than they tell you.

VR-SparseQuant: Accurate FP4 Quantization with 2:4 Sparsity on Vera Rubin
VR-SparseQuant：在Vera Rubin上使用2:4稀疏性的精确FP4量化
In recent years, GPUs have incorporated increasingly specialized support for low-precision computation, from Hopper's FP8 to Blackwell's FP4 Tensor Cores. Precision formats have evolved alongside this hardware, from MXFP4 and NVFP4 on Blackwell to E2M1 FP4 with direct UE5M3 block scaling on Vera Rubin, which we term as $\boldsymbol{VRFP4}$. These advances help serve larger language models at lower cost, but four-bit activation quantization can substantially degrade model quality. We introduce VR-SparseQuant, which uses Vera Rubin's elementwise 2:4 sparsity to recover accuracy while retaining native FP4 execution. By allowing two sparse streams to overlap, we use the same FP4 value budget as dense quantization to refine activations with larger errors. Offline scale-ratio calibration avoids runtime search, while a shared-weight GEMM accumulates both streams efficiently. On Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B, VR-SparseQuant reduces WikiText-2 perplexity from $\boldsymbol{6.85}$ to $\boldsymbol{5.57}$ versus dense RTN W4A4. Including activation preparation, our native Rubin kernels for VRFP4 achieve up to $\boldsymbol{3.1\times}$ GEMM and $\boldsymbol{2.7\times}$ MoE speedup over BF16. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study of adaptive activation quantization using Vera Rubin's elementwise 2:4 structured sparse FP4 instructions.
中文翻译
近年来，GPU逐步增加了对低精度计算的专用支持，从Hopper的FP8到Blackwell的FP4 Tensor Cores。精度格式也随之演进，从Blackwell上的MXFP4和NVFP4，到Vera Rubin上的E2M1 FP4（具有直接UE5M3块缩放），我们称之为VRFP4。这些进展有助于以更低的成本服务更大语言模型，但四比特激活量化会显著降低模型质量。我们提出VR-SparseQuant，利用Vera Rubin的逐元素2:4稀疏性，在保持原生FP4执行的同时恢复精度。通过允许两个稀疏流重叠，我们使用与密集量化相同的FP4值预算来优化误差较大的激活。离线比例校准避免了运行时搜索，而共享权重GEMM则高效地累积两个流。在Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B上，VR-SparseQuant将WikiText-2困惑度从6.85降至5.57（相比密集RTN W4A4）。包括激活准备在内，我们的原生Rubin内核在VRFP4上实现了高达3.1倍的GEMM加速和2.7倍的MoE加速。据我们所知，这是首次研究利用Vera Rubin逐元素2:4结构化稀疏FP4指令的自适应激活量化。
👤 Samir Khaki, Wei-Chen Wang, Song Han, Ryan Hanrui Wang 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.25682]
FoodReact-AR: Learning Audience Reactions that Zero-Shot LLMs Miss in Arabic Food-Insecurity Discourse
FoodReact-AR：学习零样本LLM在阿拉伯语粮食不安全话语中遗漏的受众反应
Facebook reactions record how an audience received a post, a signal absent from the post's text. We treat the six differentiated reactions on 74,241 Arabic food-insecurity posts from public Facebook pages (2020-2024) as compositions, apply a centered log-ratio transformation, and cluster them into four audience profiles: Solidarity (Love-dominant), Dismissal (Haha-dominant), Grief (Sad-dominant), and a flat Ambivalent profile. Dismissal peaks in 2022 (22.3%), the year of the wheat price shock, and grief in 2024 (14.0%), during the Gaza food emergency; a page-clustered GEE confirms both shifts (odds ratios 2.18 and 1.34), and they survive minimum-reaction filtering, alternative zero replacements and clustering methods, and bootstrap resampling. We then test whether the profiles can be predicted without reactions. A zero-shot Arabic LLM recovers Grief for 83.8% of posts but Dismissal for only 3.4%, and in a matched ablation, it shows the reaction percentages do not help. A simple classifier trained on the reaction-derived labels and tested on unseen pages recovers dismissal for 60.0% (macro-F1 0.56 against 0.43), mainly from mentions of officials and institutions. Derisive audience responses are thus learnable from text, yet a zero-shot LLM misses them. We release the corpus, LLM, and human annotation layers, and an evaluation protocol as FoodReact-AR.
中文翻译
Facebook上的反应记录了受众对帖子的接受程度，这一信号在帖子文本中并不存在。我们处理来自公共Facebook页面（2020–2024年）的74,241条阿拉伯语粮食不安全帖子的六种差异化反应，将其视为组合，应用中心对数比变换，并聚类为四种受众画像：团结型（以Love为主）、轻蔑型（以Haha为主）、哀伤型（以Sad为主）和中立型的矛盾型。轻蔑型在2022年达到峰值（22.3%），即小麦价格冲击之年；哀伤型在2024年达到峰值（14.0%），正值加沙粮食紧急状态；页面聚类的GEE分析确认了这两个变化（优势比分别为2.18和1.34），且这些结果在最低反应过滤、替代零值替换、不同聚类方法和Bootstrap重抽样下依然稳健。随后我们测试这些画像是否可在无反应的情况下被预测。一个零样本阿拉伯语LLM对83.8%的帖子正确识别出哀伤型，但仅对3.4%的帖子识别出轻蔑型；在匹配消融实验中，反应百分比并未提供帮助。一个基于反应标签训练的简单分类器在未见页面上对轻蔑型的恢复率达到60.0%（宏F1为0.56，对比0.43），主要来自对官员和机构的提及。因此，讽刺性受众反应可以从文本中学习，但零样本LLM会遗漏它们。我们发布了该语料库、LLM、人工标注层及评估协议，统称为FoodReact-AR。
👤 Wajdi Zaghouani, Mabrouka Bessghaier, Salma Azouzi, Shimaa Amer Ibrahim 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.25653]
ColorRef: Calibrating Verbal Feedback for Color Revision
ColorRef：校准颜色修改的言语反馈
People rarely specify an exact color when asking someone to revise a design: they might say \textit{a little darker} or \textit{more blue} after seeing a proposal. A language-model assistant must then infer not only which way to change the color, but how far. We study this distinction with \textsc{ColorRef}, a color reference game in which a model guesses a color from a description and a teacher, knowing a recorded target hidden from the guesser, supplies verbal corrections. On 1{,}000 crowd-sourced description--target pairs, three rounds of deterministic oracle feedback reduce mean target distance from 42.33 to 22.15 $ΔE_{76}$. However, those corrections provide additional information, and improvement alone does not establish well-calibrated feedback following. A controlled Qwen3-14B diagnostic reveals a more specific failure: following a requested direction and ordering graded phrases do not guarantee a suitable step size; strong modifiers can induce excessive changes. We therefore measure each receiver's typical response to \textit{a little}, \textit{somewhat}, and \textit{much} on separate colors and select wording matched to its observed steps. On 128 held-out starting colors, calibration reduces one-step mean error from 12.36 to 6.86 for Qwen3-14B and from 11.06 to 5.89 for Gemma 4 12B against a fixed graded-wording rule. The benefit is concentrated on large corrections, with many unchanged cases and no consistent increase in threshold success. Our findings show that following the direction of feedback is not the same as making an appropriately sized revision.
中文翻译
人们在要求他人修改设计时很少指定确切颜色：他们可能会说“稍微暗一点”或“更蓝一些”来回应提案。语言模型助手必须推断不仅改变方向，还要判断改变幅度。我们通过 extsc{ColorRef}——一种颜色参考游戏——研究这一区别，其中模型根据描述猜测颜色，教师知道隐藏的目标颜色，提供言语修正。在1,000个众包生成的描述-目标对上，三轮确定性Oracle反馈将平均目标距离从42.33降至22.15 $ΔE_{76}$。然而，这些修正提供了额外信息，仅改善并不足以说明反馈是良好校准的。一项针对Qwen3-14B的受控诊断揭示了更具体的问题：遵循请求的方向和分级短语并不保证合适的步长；强修饰词可能导致过度修改。因此，我们分别测量接收者对“一点”、“有些”和“很多”等词语在不同颜色上的典型响应，并选择与其实际步长匹配的措辞。在128个保留的起始颜色上，校准将Qwen3-14B的一步平均误差从12.36降至6.86，Gemma 4 12B从11.06降至5.89，优于固定分级用语规则。收益集中在大修正上，许多情况无变化，且没有一致的阈值成功率提升。我们的发现表明，遵循反馈方向并不等同于做出适当大小的修改。
👤 Pablo Loyola, Andres Hoyos-Idrobo, Edison Marrese-Taylor, Colin Rowat, Alfredo Solano 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.25643]
TRAC: Efficient Diffusion Language Models for Machine Translation via Length-Aware Decoding
TRAC：通过长度感知解码实现机器翻译的高效扩散语言模型
Machine translation with large language models requires both high translation quality and low generation latency. Accelerating translation while preserving quality is therefore essential for practical deployment. Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) can generate tokens at arbitrary target positions through iterative denoising, offering a new paradigm for parallel translation beyond sequential autoregressive decoding. However, existing methods still incur redundant computation: fixed target generation lengths waste computation on unused positions, while confidence-only commitment delays medium-confidence tokens whose predictions have largely stabilized. To address these issues, we propose TRAC, a training-free adaptive decoding method for diffusion machine translation. TRAC adapts the target generation length using source length and translation direction, with a conservative fallback to the maximum length for empty or unusually short outputs. It preserves high-confidence commitment while identifying temporally stable medium-confidence tokens for early commitment using cross-step top-1 persistence and approximate Jensen-Shannon divergence over Top-$K$ distributions. On bidirectional Chinese-English translation across FLORES-200, WMT23, and NTREX-128, TRAC reduces model forward calls by 24.9% to 36.8% and achieves sentence-level generation speedups of 1.91 to 2.19 times over Fast-dLLM, with only a 0.02-point decrease in macro-average COMET. These results demonstrate that adaptive generation lengths and stability-aware token commitment improve the efficiency of diffusion machine translation.
中文翻译
使用大型语言模型进行机器翻译需要高翻译质量和低生成延迟。在保持质量的前提下加速翻译对于实际部署至关重要。扩散型大型语言模型（dLLMs）可通过迭代去噪在任意目标位置生成标记，为超越顺序自回归解码提供了新的并行翻译范式。然而，现有方法仍存在冗余计算问题：固定的目标生成长度会浪费未使用位置的计算资源，而仅基于置信度的承诺机制会延迟那些预测已基本稳定的中等置信度标记。为解决这些问题，我们提出TRAC，一种无需训练的扩散机器翻译自适应解码方法。TRAC根据源文本长度和翻译方向动态调整目标生成长度，并在输出为空或异常短时采用保守的最大长度回退策略。它在保留高置信度承诺的同时，利用跨步Top-1一致性与Top-K分布的近似Jensen-Shannon散度识别时间上稳定的中等置信度标记以实现早期承诺。在FLORES-200、WMT23和NTREX-128上的双向中英翻译任务中，TRAC将模型前向调用减少24.9%至36.8%，相比Fast-dLLM实现了1.91至2.19倍的句子级生成速度提升，仅导致宏平均COMET分数下降0.02分。结果表明，自适应生成长度与稳定性感知的标记承诺可显著提升扩散机器翻译的效率。
👤 Tianyuan Li, Xihan Yang, Chenglong Wang, Yuzhang Wu, Jingbo Zhu et al. 📅 2026-11-19 🔗 arXiv
[2611.24981]
Quantifying Cultural-Psychological Constructs in Four Centuries of Ottoman Court Registers
量化四百年奥斯曼法院记录中的文化-心理构念
Populations vary along cultural dimensions such as individualism, collectivism, and honor. But how can these dimensions be studied in historical societies for which conventional psychological data are unavailable? Here, we introduce a computational text-analytic framework for quantifying psychological constructs in 54,670 Ottoman Turkish court records from 21 Istanbul courts spanning 1513-1911 CE. We develop sentence-embedding models adapted to Ottoman legal language and extend a theory-driven, text-analytic pipeline to capture cultural variation along the dimensions of individualism, collectivism, and honor in this historical corpus. We evaluate our models through multiple methods: document retrieval, semantic textual similarity, questionnaire-item classification, and validation against expert annotations. Our adapted sentence transformer (OTA-BERTurk) achieved a Recall@1 of .843 on the test set for document retrieval, a Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) of .806 for semantic textual similarity (meaning the best answer usually appeared as a top result), and classified questionnaire items with 89.58\% accuracy. Document-level performance varied across constructs and methods. Qualitative analysis further showed that high scores reflected historically specific legal and institutional patterns. We publicly release the models, construct representations, scoring pipeline, as well as an interactive web interface to support transparent, reproducible historical-psychological analyses of Ottoman Turkish court records spanning approximately four centuries. These computational tools enable the study of psychological variation across centuries of cultural and institutional change in a diverse empire.
中文翻译
人口在个体主义、集体主义和荣誉等文化维度上存在差异。但对于缺乏传统心理学数据的历史社会，如何研究这些维度？本文提出一种计算文本分析框架，用于量化1513–1911年期间来自21个伊斯坦布尔法院的54,670份奥斯曼土耳其语法院记录中的心理构念。我们开发了适配奥斯曼法律语言的句子嵌入模型，并扩展了一个理论驱动的文本分析流程，以捕捉该历史语料库中个体主义、集体主义和荣誉的文化变异。我们通过多种方法评估模型：文档检索、语义文本相似性、问卷项目分类及与专家标注的对比验证。我们适配的句子变换器（OTA-BERTurk）在文档检索测试集上达到Recall@1为0.843，语义文本相似性上平均倒数排名（MRR）为0.806（意味着最佳答案通常位列前列），问卷项目分类准确率达89.58%。文档级性能在不同构念和方法间有所差异。定性分析进一步显示，高分反映了特定历史时期的法律与制度模式。我们公开发布模型、构念表示、评分流程及交互式网页界面，以支持透明、可复现的奥斯曼土耳其法院记录跨四世纪的心理学历史分析。这些计算工具使我们能够研究一个多元帝国在数个世纪的文化与制度变迁中，心理变异的演变。

TETHER: Training-Free, Elastic, ToC-Anchored Hierarchy Extraction and Recovery in Design-Driven Long Documents
TETHER：无需训练、弹性、基于目录锚定的结构化层次提取与恢复，适用于设计驱动型长文档
Converting complex documents into structured knowledge constitutes the critical infrastructure urgently required for deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) in downstream research. However, blind to the Table of Contents (ToC) as a macro-structure, existing bottom-up paradigms rely on localized visual cues, inducing heuristic fragility in high-entropy layouts where visual prominence decouples from logical depth; meanwhile, direct LLM extraction lacks spatial grounding, triggering hallucinations in long contexts. To address this, we introduce TETHER, a training-free framework that elevates the ToC from discarded noise to a vital structural prior, enabling robust adaptation across design-driven long documents. TETHER operates via a two-stage neuro-symbolic mechanism: first, it constructs a document skeleton from the raw ToC using a Large Multimodal Model (LMM) empowered by spatio-semantic constraints; second, it employs an anchor-based alignment strategy to tether content blocks to this skeleton, effectively resolving boundary ambiguities. Evaluated across a multilingual benchmark of ESG reports, product manuals, and annual reports, TETHER delivers highly precise, state-of-the-art (SOTA) zero-shot hierarchy recovery, outperforming deep hierarchical recovery models, dedicated parsers, and general-purpose LMMs. Moreover, stress tests highlight TETHER's resilience to sparse omissions and textual noise, demonstrating that its precise semantic alignment is fundamentally anchored in topological and depth fidelity. Crucially, ablations confirm the framework's architectural irreducibility, while achieving 90% token reduction and drastic speedups over end-to-end baselines, establishing a compelling equilibrium between structural precision and deployment scalability.
中文翻译
将复杂文档转化为结构化知识，是部署大型语言模型（LLMs）于下游研究的关键基础设施。然而，现有自底向上的范式忽视了目录（ToC）作为宏观结构的作用，依赖局部视觉线索，导致在高熵布局中因视觉显著性与逻辑深度脱钩而产生启发式脆弱性；同时，直接使用LLM进行提取缺乏空间定位，引发长上下文中的幻觉问题。为此，我们提出TETHER，一种无需训练的框架，将目录从被丢弃的噪声提升为关键的结构先验，从而在设计驱动型长文档中实现稳健适应。TETHER采用双阶段神经符号机制：首先，利用大模型（LMM）结合空间语义约束，从原始目录构建文档骨架；其次，采用基于锚点的对齐策略，将内容块绑定至该骨架，有效解决边界模糊问题。在涵盖ESG报告、产品手册和年报的多语言基准上评估，TETHER实现了高度精确、领先水平的零样本层次恢复，优于深度层次恢复模型、专用解析器及通用LMM。压力测试表明，TETHER对稀疏缺失和文本噪声具有强鲁棒性，证明其精确的语义对齐本质上根植于拓扑和深度保真度。关键的是，消融实验证明该框架的架构不可简化，同时相比端到端基线实现90%的token减少和显著提速，建立了结构精度与部署可扩展性之间的有力平衡。
