Hybrid Retrieval for Government Scheme Search in English and Marathi
面向英语与马拉地语的政府计划搜索的混合检索
Retrieving government schemes requires matching citizens' information needs to formal administrative descriptions, often across languages and terminology. We present a bilingual search system for Maharashtra government schemes and public services that combines BM25 retrieval with sentence-embedding similarity through equal-weight score fusion. The system indexes aligned English-Marathi scheme descriptions and supports multilingual, Marathi-focused, and translation-mediated semantic configurations. Evaluation on manually curated bilingual queries compares BM25, dense retrieval, and hybrid retrieval using IndicSBERT and MahaSBERT. BM25 provides a strong baseline with a mean reciprocal rank (MRR) of 0.864. Hybrid retrieval increases MRR to 0.872 with IndicSBERT and 0.879 with MahaSBERT, whereas the corresponding dense-only systems achieve 0.663 and 0.655. MahaSBERT-based fusion also increases precision from 0.781 to 0.808 while matching BM25 recall at 0.946. These results support the complementary use of lexical and semantic signals in this collection, while showing that improvements over a strong lexical baseline are modest and metric-dependent. The study provides an application-focused analysis of hybrid retrieval for bilingual public-service information access.
中文翻译
政府计划的检索需要将公民的信息需求与正式的行政描述相匹配，通常涉及跨语言和术语差异。我们提出了一种针对马哈拉施特拉邦政府计划和公共服务的双语搜索系统，该系统通过等权重得分融合的方式结合了BM25检索与句子嵌入相似性。系统对齐了英文-马拉地语的计划描述，并支持多语言、以马拉地语为中心以及翻译媒介的语义配置。在人工标注的双语查询上进行评估，比较了BM25、密集检索和混合检索方法（使用IndicSBERT和MahaSBERT）。BM25提供了强大的基线，平均倒数排名（MRR）为0.864。混合检索使用IndicSBERT时将MRR提升至0.872，使用MahaSBERT时提升至0.879，而对应的纯密集系统分别仅达到0.663和0.655。基于MahaSBERT的融合还使精确率从0.781提升至0.808，同时保持与BM25相当的召回率（0.946）。这些结果支持在该数据集中词汇与语义信号的互补使用，同时也表明在强词汇基线上取得的改进有限且依赖于具体指标。本研究为双语公共服务信息访问中的混合检索提供了应用导向的分析。

[2611.26059]
Complete-SVD: A Functional Norm Approach to Low-Rank LLM Compression
Complete-SVD：一种用于低秩大语言模型压缩的功能范数方法
Post-training low-rank compression of neural networks must not only approximate the original weights but also preserve the model's input-output behavior. Recent output-aware methods measure the compression error beyond the compressed layer, at the model output, weighting logit changes by the observed next token or by the model's predicted probabilities. We propose Complete-SVD, a method for large language models that minimizes the $L_2$ distance between the logits of the original and compressed models, weighting all logit changes equally. Linearizing the network around the original weights, together with a Kronecker-factored approximation, reduces this objective to a weighted SVD with a closed-form solution. Its output-side factor is intractable to compute; we estimate it without bias from random probes, at a cost of $K$ backward passes per sequence, independent of vocabulary size and sequence length. At matched settings with uniform ranks and WikiText-2 calibration, Complete-SVD achieves the lowest C4 and PTB perplexity in every setting, and at ratio 0.7 under LEMS rank allocation. Replacing the $L_2$ distance by the Kullback-Leibler divergence (KL-SVD), with the same estimation framework, lowers calibration perplexity in most settings and raises downstream accuracy, but raises cross-corpus perplexity. Complete-SVD also improves IFEval instruction-following accuracy over the strongest prior output-aware methods throughout.
中文翻译
后训练阶段的神经网络低秩压缩不仅需近似原始权重，还需保留模型的输入输出行为。近期的输出感知方法在模型输出处衡量压缩误差，通过观测到的下一个词或模型预测概率加权logit变化。我们提出Complete-SVD，一种针对大语言模型的方法，其最小化原始模型与压缩模型之间logit的$L_2$距离，并对所有logit变化同等加权。通过在线性化网络围绕原始权重并采用Kronecker分解近似，该目标简化为一个带闭式解的加权SVD。其输出侧因子难以计算；我们通过随机探针无偏估计，代价为每序列$K$次反向传播，与词汇量和序列长度无关。在匹配设置下（统一秩和WikiText-2校准），Complete-SVD在所有设置中均取得最低的C4和PTB困惑度，且在LEMS秩分配比例0.7时表现最优。将$L_2$距离替换为Kullback-Leibler散度（KL-SVD），在相同估计框架下，多数设置中降低了校准困惑度并提高了下游准确率，但增加了跨语料库困惑度。Complete-SVD还在整个范围内提升了IFEval指令遵循准确率，超越最强的先前输出感知方法。

[2611.25960]
When Sounds Turn Heads: Recovering Event Timing That Audio LLMs Fail to Say
当声音吸引注意力时：恢复音频大模型未能表达的事件时间
Audio LLMs can know when a queried sound occurs but fail to say it. We find a few attention heads that track the queried sound: they shift when the query changes, follow the sound when it moves, and removing or blocking them often hurts the model's timestamps. Yet the answer does not express what they track. Intervals read directly from these heads outperform the model's original answer and recover events that its timestamps miss. To bring this timing into the answer, we introduce head-guided decoding, which needs no training. It keeps the model's decision on whether the sound is present and rescores its candidate timestamps with a prior from these heads. Heads selected on development data transfer unchanged to new recordings and free-text queries, where head-guided decoding improves the timestamps of three audio LLMs, scores above their head readout on every benchmark, and, in most settings, outperforms random-head guidance. On recordings released after the models were trained, head-guided decoding raises mIoU from 0.07-0.36 to 0.48-0.56. These results suggest that poor temporal answers in audio LLMs partly reflect a failure to say rather than to hear, and that the model's own attention can close part of this gap.
中文翻译
音频大模型能够知道查询声音发生的时间，但无法准确表达出来。我们发现少数注意力头会追踪所查询的声音：当查询改变时它们会发生偏移，当声音移动时它们会随之移动，而移除或阻断这些头通常会损害模型的时间戳表现。然而，模型的答案并未反映这些头所追踪的内容。直接从这些头读取的时间间隔优于模型原始答案，并能恢复其时间戳遗漏的事件。为将这一时间信息引入答案，我们提出了头引导解码，该方法无需训练。它保留模型对声音是否存在判断，用这些头的先验对候选时间戳重新评分。在开发数据上选定的头可直接应用于新录音和自由文本查询，头引导解码提升了三个音频大模型的时间戳表现，在每个基准测试上得分均高于头读出结果，并在大多数设置中优于随机头引导。在模型训练后发布的录音上，头引导解码使mIoU从0.07-0.36提升至0.48-0.56。这些结果表明，音频大模型时间回答不佳部分源于‘无法说出’而非‘听不到’，且模型自身的注意力机制可部分弥合这一差距。

[2611.25895]
One Schedule Does Not Fit All: Adaptive Inference Schedules for Diffusion Language Models
一个调度不适合所有情况：扩散语言模型的自适应推理调度
Diffusion language models typically use a fixed denoising schedule across workloads. We show that the cheapest schedule that preserves quality depends on the task, model, and sampler. We propose Task-Calibrated Denoising Allocation (TCDA), a deployment rule with an explicit error guarantee. Given candidate samplers, paired calibration data, a utility tolerance, and guardrails against degeneration, TCDA uses simultaneous one-sided bootstrap bounds to certify which candidates meet both the utility and guardrail criteria. It deploys the cheapest certified candidate or falls back to the full schedule. The probability of deploying an infeasible schedule is asymptotically at most alpha. Candidates include early prefix decoding, state-reusing continuation, reduced time grids, and confidence-threshold decoding. Across seven datasets and two model families, TCDA certifies reductions in denoiser evaluations by factors of 3.4 on XSum and 2.0 on WMT14 for ELF-B; 8.0 on WMT14 and 4.1 on GSM8K for LLaDA-8B; and 1.33 on OpenWebText for both ELF-M and ELF-L. A validation protocol fixed before generation confirmed every selection on fresh seeds. Across 500 calibration splits, false certification remained below 2% in every setting at a nominal level of 5%. Certified schedules vary across tasks and model scales. Trajectory measurements suggest that when decoded tokens commit is more informative for schedule selection than the remaining latent discretization error. We also find that the ELF reference sampler draws its time grid at random, so reproducible schedule comparisons for unconditional generation require fixing the grid.
中文翻译
扩散语言模型通常在不同工作负载中使用固定的去噪调度。我们发现，保持质量的最廉价调度取决于任务、模型和采样器。我们提出了任务校准去噪分配（TCDA），一种具有显式误差保证的部署规则。给定候选采样器、配对校准数据、效用容差以及防止退化的约束条件，TCDA使用单侧Bootstrap置信区间同时验证候选方案是否满足效用和约束标准。它部署最廉价的合格候选方案，或退回到完整调度。部署不可行调度的概率渐近不超过α。候选方案包括早期前缀解码、状态复用续写、减少时间网格和置信度阈值解码。在七个数据集和两个模型家族上，TCDA在XSum上实现去噪器评估次数减少3.4倍，在WMT14上减少2.0倍（ELF-B）；在WMT14上减少8.0倍，在GSM8K上减少4.1倍（LLaDA-8B）；在OpenWebText上减少1.33倍（ELF-M和ELF-L）。在生成前固定验证协议并使用新鲜种子确认了每次选择。在500个校准划分中，所有设置下误认证率均低于2%（名义水平5%）。经认证的调度随任务和模型规模变化。轨迹测量表明，解码标记的确定性比剩余潜在离散化误差更适合作为调度选择的依据。我们还发现，ELF参考采样器随机生成时间网格，因此无条件生成的可重复调度比较需要固定网格。

Anchor and Check: Deterministic Cross-Prompt, Cross-Model Reconciliation for Auditable LLM Decisions
锚定与核查：用于可审计大模型决策的确定性跨提示、跨模型协调
Pipelines that let a large language model (LLM) make discrete decisions need to know which decisions they may act on unattended, and a record that explains each one. We study a small, fully specified reconciliation contract: commit the target model's greedy answer (the anchor), draw one or two greedy checks that reach the answer by a different route (an alternate prompt, or a model from another family), and let a pure function return one of five outcomes, of which only Agree is acted on. On 48,000 model-item evaluations over 6,000 questions, with every method given access to verbalized confidence as a tie-breaker, the main finding concerns where calls come from: pooling another family's samples raises semantic entropy's AUROC by 1.7 to 4.0 points at every budget, and a same-model temperature check is the weakest design. As complete pipelines, the point estimates of the three-call contract and pooled semantic entropy over 3 or 5 samples differ by less than one point, with intervals containing zero. As detectors of the anchor's errors, the contract is ahead of pooled entropy with 3 and 5 samples under both tie-breaker conventions; with 10 samples the point estimates differ by less than one point and the sign depends on the convention. Under the strict Agree gate it automates 70% of items and still acts on 16% of errors. What the contract adds is a fixed committed answer, an explicit outcome and a decision replayable from its record; generation itself was not reproducible on the hosted API.

允许大语言模型（LLM）做出离散决策的流水线需要知道哪些决策可能在无人监督下被执行，以及每项决策的解释记录。我们研究了一个小型、完全指定的协调契约：承诺目标模型的贪婪答案（锚点），通过不同路径（另一种提示或另一家族的模型）生成一个或两个贪婪检查，并让纯函数返回五种结果之一，其中只有“同意”会被执行。在6,000个问题上的48,000次模型-项目评估中，每种方法均可访问口头置信度作为决胜手段，主要发现集中在调用来源：使用另一家族样本池化可使语义熵的AUROC在每个预算下提高1.7至4.0分，而同模型温度检查是设计中最弱的。作为完整流水线，三调用契约与3或5个样本的池化语义熵的点估计差异小于1分，置信区间包含零。作为锚点错误检测器，该契约在两种决胜约定下均优于池化熵（3和5个样本）；在10个样本时，点估计差异小于1分，符号取决于约定。在严格的“同意”门控下，该契约自动化了70%的项目，但仍对16%的错误作出响应。该契约带来的价值在于固定承诺的答案、明确的结果以及可从记录重播的决策；在托管API上，生成本身不可重现。

What Does Anti-Hallucination Training Buy? Saying Less, Cheap Pruning, and the Rest
反幻觉训练到底带来了什么？少说一点、廉价剪枝与其余部分
Preference training is the standard recipe for reducing object hallucination in vision-language models, and each new method reports a lower CHAIR score. We ask what that reduction consists of. Every trained system is compared with its base model's caption truncated to the same object recall, which removes the part of the gain that comes from saying less; the remainder is compared with what a deliberately weak, frozen, eleven-feature post-hoc probe fitted on 500 annotated images can reproduce. All criteria were specified in the analysis code and frozen before the outputs they judge existed. For eight public methods on LLaVA-1.5-7B, the probe reproduces at least two thirds of what training removes beyond saying less for 7 of 8 methods on COCO, 8 of 8 on AMBER and 6 of 8 on Object HalBench, with RLAIF-V the consistent exception; a later public method released for both LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen2.5-VL (SENTINEL) meets the same bar on both. On our own DPO checkpoints the accounting holds on Qwen2.5-VL but fails on InternVL2.5 on COCO (2 of 4 training designs). At matched recall, the base model plus the probe beats truncation for 8 of 9 LLaVA-1.5 systems on COCO and 9 of 9 on AMBER, on the two newer backbones, and when realized as actual sentence deletion. The same measure prices training signals: DPO on the model's own samples labelled by the probe or by an object-level CLIP score recovers 0.70 and 0.87 of the beyond-brevity gain of ground-truth-labelled DPO on Qwen2.5-VL (the probe 0.73 on InternVL2.5), both beat length-matched random labels, and CLIP labels buy more than probe labels on COCO. Human judgements show that the sentences the probe deletes are wrong more often than those truncation would delete; a human check of the CHAIR evaluator's precision falls below our pre-set bar, which we report as a limitation.

偏好训练是减少视觉语言模型对象幻觉的标准方法，每种新方法都报告更低的CHAIR分数。我们探讨这种降低究竟由什么构成。每个训练系统均与基线模型在相同对象召回率下的截断字幕进行比较，以去除因‘说得更少’带来的收益部分；剩余部分再与一个故意弱化、冻结的十一特征事后探测器（在500张标注图像上拟合）所能再现的效果进行比较。所有标准均在分析代码中预先设定并冻结，早于被评估输出的存在。在LLaVA-1.5-7B上的八种公开方法中，探测器在COCO上至少复现了8种方法中7种、AMBER上全部8种、Object HalBench上6种（RLAIF-V为例外）；后来发布的公开方法SENTINEL在LLaVA-1.5和Qwen2.5-VL上均达到相同标准。在我们自己的DPO检查点上，该会计框架在Qwen2.5-VL上成立，但在InternVL2.5的COCO上失败（4种训练设计中2种）。在匹配召回率下，基线模型加探测器在COCO上击败截断法（9种LLaVA-1.5系统中8种）、AMBER上全部9种，且在较新骨干网络上也成立，实际表现为句子删除。同一衡量标准也揭示了训练信号的价值：在模型自身样本上使用探测器或对象级CLIP得分标注的DPO，分别恢复了真实标注DPO在Qwen2.5-VL上超越简洁性的0.70和0.87倍收益（探测器在InternVL2.5上为0.73），两者均优于长度匹配的随机标签，且CLIP标签在COCO上比探测器标签带来更多收益。人类判断显示，探测器删除的句子比截断法删除的句子更常出错；人类对CHAIR评估器精度的检查低于我们预设的基准，我们将其列为局限性。

[2611.25672]
SEAF: Selective Semantic Enrichment and Visual Evidence Fusion for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection
SEAF：用于多模态讽刺检测的选择性语义增强与视觉证据融合
Multimodal sarcasm detection requires textual, visual, and contextual evidence, particularly when multiple images provide complementary cues. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate auxiliary information from a post's text and images, but their outputs may mix useful background knowledge with redundant visual descriptions. We propose SEAF, a dual-branch framework for selective semantic enrichment and visual evidence fusion. SEAF assigns generated fields to distinct roles: entity background enriches semantic input, while image descriptions supply alignment statistics. The semantic branch combines the original post text, background from a frozen MLLM, and optical character recognition (OCR) text. The aligned visual branch combines global and local visual features with statistics of cross-modal and inter-image similarity. Text-Anchored Smoothed Aggregation (TASA) weights images by their similarity to the original post text while preserving contributions from images with low literal similarity. Summing the branch classification scores integrates contextual semantics with visual evidence. Experiments on the multi-image MMSD3.0 benchmark show that SEAF outperforms all compared methods.
中文翻译

When Should a Voice Agent Speak? End-of-Turn Detection in Turkish
语音智能体何时应发言？土耳其语中的对话回合结束检测
A voice agent that waits for a fixed silence before replying either interrupts users who pause mid-utterance or responds late to users who have finished. This study examines the decision at the onset of silence, using turn ends and turn-internal pauses extracted from the Real-TurnTurk corpus of Turkish dyadic video calls. A fine-tuned Turkish TurnGPT model, a zero-shot Turkish language model and a deployed multilingual detector are compared, together with voice-activity timing and facial features, under recording-disjoint and speaker-disjoint evaluation with paired cluster bootstrap tests. On held-out recordings, the fine-tuned model separates turn ends from pauses better than both text baselines and is the best calibrated of the three. Timing features of the current speaker are similarly informative, and their combination with the fine-tuned text score gives the best timing-based results; with logistic regression, this combination also outperforms the same combination with either text baseline. In a latency simulation, adding the text score to a silence timeout shortens the mean response delay at the same interruption rate. On automatic Whisper transcripts, the fine-tuned model retains its advantage over the zero-shot model.

[2611.25247]
Retrieving Patristic References and Modelling Topics in Bullinger's Reformation Correspondence
从布尔林格改革通信中检索教父引文并建模主题
The correspondence of Heinrich Bullinger (1504-1575), Swiss Reformer and Antistes of Zurich, documents the political and religious debates of the Reformation, including extensive appeals to the Church Fathers as authoritative sources. Detecting such references computationally remains largely unexplored. We investigate dense and sparse retrieval for patristic reference detection, and metadata-informed topic modelling as a proxy for the topics and ideas discussed. Prior work on Latin intertextuality has fine-tuned dense retrievers for their respective target domain, and we test how well these models transfer to Bullinger's correspondence. We compare lexical (BM25), dense (bi-encoders), and hybrid methods on explicit and implicit references. A Latin encoder fine-tuned on biblical reuse performs best among the bi-encoders (82%, Recall@20 on explicit and 16% on implicit references), while BM25 outperforms dense approaches on explicit citations (94%, R@20). Because BM25 and dense retrieval recover disjoint sets of implicit references, fusing their rankings raises implicit R@20 to 22%, close to cross-encoder reranking of the same candidates (26%) but without its inference cost. For topic modelling, we extend FASTopic with a seed-guided loss function and editorial abstract embeddings. Measurable coherence gains over the baseline remain minimal, but qualitative and temporal analysis shows that some topics track documented historical events. However, dominant topic weights barely exceed a uniform distribution, making topics unusable to inform retrieval. A pilot application of LLM-based concept induction (LLooM) further points towards a more human-interpretable alternative. Together, these results present a first approach to retrieving patristic references and modelling fine-grained topics in Bullinger's correspondence.
中文翻译
海因里希·布尔林格（1504–1575）——瑞士改革家及苏黎世教会领袖——的通信记录了宗教改革时期的政治与宗教辩论，其中大量引用教父作为权威来源。计算检测此类引文仍基本未被探索。我们研究了密集检索与稀疏检索在教父引文检测中的应用，以及基于元数据的主题建模作为所讨论主题与思想的代理。先前关于拉丁语互文性的研究已针对特定目标领域微调了密集检索器，我们测试这些模型在布尔林格通信中的迁移效果。我们在显性和隐性引文上对比了词法（BM25）、密集（双编码器）和混合方法。在双编码器中，基于圣经再利用微调的拉丁语编码器表现最佳（显性引文Recall@20为82%，隐性引文为16%）；而在显性引用上，BM25优于密集方法（R@20为94%）。由于BM25与密集检索在隐性引文上召回集合不重叠，融合其排名可将隐性R@20提升至22%，接近交叉编码器重排序的结果（26%），且无需其推理开销。在主题建模方面，我们扩展了FASTopic，引入种子引导损失函数和编辑摘要嵌入。虽然与基线相比可衡量的连贯性提升有限，但定性和时间分析显示部分主题能追踪到已知历史事件。然而，主导主题权重几乎未超过均匀分布，导致主题无法用于指导检索。一项基于LLM的概念归纳（LLooM）试点应用进一步指向更具人类可解释性的替代方案。总体而言，这些结果构成了首次尝试在布尔林格通信中检索教父引文并建模细粒度主题的方法。

Living off the Local Model: Abusing Browser-Embedded Language Models for Post-Exploitation
依赖本地模型生存：滥用浏览器嵌入式语言模型进行后渗透攻击
Browsers now ship local language models to ordinary endpoints. Google includes Gemini Nano in Chrome to enable productivity features such as summarization, with inference running entirely on the device. These browser-embedded models provide new living-off-the-land opportunities for attackers, each instance a potential post-exploitation engine already installed, signed by the vendor, and invisible to network monitoring. The Gemini model is multimodal, capable of processing files, analyzing screenshots, or conversing with an attacker. We show that it can be driven from the host using only interpreters that ship with the operating system and find that the one-time user-activation check before the model is downloaded is easily satisfied with a simulated click or bypassed entirely.We measure and report the current model's ability to identify credentials on the host, detect PII in email, classify images, and call custom tools. We combine these capabilities and use the model to carry out a simulated cyber operation: reading a password from a webcam image, unlocking an encrypted archive, triaging the contents, and staging a private key for exfiltration, end-to-end without user-interaction.
中文翻译
现代浏览器现在将本地语言模型部署到普通终端设备。谷歌在Chrome中集成Gemini Nano以支持摘要等生产力功能，推理完全在设备上运行。这些嵌入浏览器的语言模型为攻击者提供了新的“就地生存”机会，每个实例都是一个已安装、由厂商签名且网络监控无法察觉的潜在后渗透引擎。Gemini模型具备多模态能力，可处理文件、分析截图或与攻击者对话。我们展示仅使用操作系统自带的解释器即可驱动该模型，并发现模型下载前的一次性用户激活检查可通过模拟点击轻松满足，或直接绕过。我们测量并报告当前模型在主机上识别凭证、检测邮件中的PII、图像分类以及调用自定义工具的能力。我们结合这些能力，利用模型执行一次模拟网络行动：从摄像头图像中读取密码，解锁加密归档，分类内容，并准备私钥以进行外泄，全程无需用户交互。
👤 William Fleshman, Joseph Lucas 📅 2026-11-18 🔗 arXiv
[2611.25003]
A Model of Diverse Sampling from Language Models
语言模型中多样化采样的模型
Language models often produce high-quality individual samples but poor sets, with repeated samples clustering around the same semantic modes. We formalize diverse generation as sampling a size-$K$ subset of complete strings from a determinantal point process (DPP), where the likelihood encodes item quality, and the embedding geometry encodes repulsion between similar outputs. Exact inference over all strings is intractable, so we propose PoolDPP: draw a finite candidate pool from a tractable proposal, importance-weight the candidates toward the target quality, and run a $K$-DPP sampler on the induced pool kernel; a greedy MAP variant provides deterministic selection. We prove a finite-sample bound with an explicit constant: the KL divergence between the sampler's output and the target $K$-DPP decays as O(1/N) in the pool size. Empirically, PoolDPP extends the quality--diversity frontier on open-ended generation and the precision--recall frontier on ambiguous text-to-SQL (Ambrosia), and on mathematical reasoning (GSM8K), it raises diversity at about the same pass@$K$.
中文翻译
语言模型通常能生成高质量的单个样本，但生成的样本集合质量较差，重复样本会聚集在相同的语义模式附近。我们将多样化生成形式化为从确定性点过程（DPP）中采样大小为$K$的完整字符串子集，其中似然度编码了项目质量，嵌入几何结构编码了相似输出之间的排斥作用。对所有字符串进行精确推断是不可行的，因此我们提出PoolDPP：从一个可处理的提议分布中抽取有限的候选池，对候选样本进行重要性加权以逼近目标质量，并在诱导的池核上运行$K$-DPP采样器；其贪心MAP变体提供确定性选择。我们证明了一个具有显式常数的有限样本界：采样器输出与目标$K$-DPP之间的KL散度随池大小呈O(1/N)衰减。实验表明，PoolDPP在开放式生成任务中扩展了质量-多样性前沿，在模糊文本到SQL任务（Ambrosia）中扩展了精确率-召回率前沿，并在数学推理任务（GSM8K）中提升了多样性，同时保持相近的pass@$K$性能。
👤 Manuel Prada-Corral, Yahya Emara, Timothy J. O'Donnell, Ryan Cotterell, Tim Vieira 📅 2026-11-18 🔗 arXiv
